We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [172] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在 Lean 定理证明器中验证约束规划问题的框架，同时覆盖建模层面的语义保持性证明（如等价性、等满足性、对称性破坏约束的正确性）和求解器层面的证书检查（通过翻译到 MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB 等外部格式）。该框架实现了端到端的可信工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可确立问题的可满足性或不可满足性。实验表明，经验证的对称性破坏在参数化证明一次后可跨所有实例规模复用，将求解器搜索工作量降低高达 2×10^7 倍，且 Lean 内部认证时间在最大实例上仅需几分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束规划广泛应用于调度、规划、配置和验证等复杂组合问题，但其可信度要求在两个层面得到保证：一是预处理重述必须保持语义，二是求解器必须给出正确答案。现有工具链通常缺乏对这两个层面的形式化验证，导致结果难以完全信任。

Method: 在 Lean 定理证明器中构建统一框架：1) 面向问题族的参数化证明，验证建模层面的等价性、等满足性及对称性破坏约束的正确性；2) 将求解器产生的证书通过后端翻译为 MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB 等外部格式，在 Lean 中进行证书检查；3) 结合两层验证形成端到端工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可确立可满足性或不可满足性。

Result: 实验显示，单次参数化对称性破坏证明即可跨所有实例规模复用，将求解器搜索工作量降低高达 2×10^7 倍；整个 Lean 内部认证过程在最大实例上耗时仅几分钟，证明了框架的实用性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在定理证明器中实现了约束规划问题的端到端形式化验证，同时兼顾建模正确性与求解器正确性，且经验证的对称性破坏带来显著的性能提升，认证开销可控，为可信约束求解提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [173] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EndoCLIP，一种结肠镜视觉语言基础模型，通过从 28 万份常规结肠镜报告中恢复 12.5 万对病灶级图像-文本配对进行训练，在病灶级检索、结构化报告生成及六项多中心临床分类任务上均优于现有通用和生物医学视觉语言编码器，其线性探针在良恶性分类上逼近 12 位专家内镜医师的盲测表现，表明将临床发现与图像帧对齐可将常规文档转化为可扩展监督，实现以语言而非任务特定标注指定临床目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规结肠镜报告包含丰富的专家描述（病灶外观、大小、位置），但仅总结整个手术过程而非单帧图像，导致临床发现与对应图像仅弱关联，限制了视觉语言模型在结肠镜领域的应用。

Method: 构建 EndoCLIP：从 280,476 份常规结肠镜记录中渐进式恢复 125,756 对病灶级图像-文本配对，训练结肠镜专用视觉语言基础模型；在病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成及六项多中心临床分类任务上进行零样本与线性探针评估，并与通用及生物医学视觉语言编码器对比；另开展 12 位内镜医师盲测研究对比良恶性分类性能。

Result: EndoCLIP 在所有评估任务中均显著优于现有通用和生物医学视觉语言编码器；在良恶性分类任务中，其线性探针性能接近 12 位专家内镜医师的盲测水平。

Conclusion: 通过恢复临床发现到图像帧的对应关系，可将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能通过自然语言指定，而非为每个任务单独标注，为结肠镜及其他医学影像领域的视觉语言建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [174] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，解决纳卫星下行带宽受限及飞机目标数据集稀缺、类别不平衡的问题，实现了实时自主空中监视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳卫星下行带宽有限，传统“弯管”架构需传输海量原始影像至地面处理，导致决策延迟；现有开放卫星飞机数据集稀缺且严重类别不平衡，阻碍检测器学习稀有类别的鲁棒表征。

Method: 在配备低功耗边缘张量加速器的 6U CubeSat 上执行星上推理；采用低秩适应微调扩散模型生成合成少数类影像，经中间检测器自动伪标注后与传统增强样本融合，构建平衡数据集训练量化检测器。

Result: 平衡数据集使全局 mAP 从 77.9% 提升至 82.2%，少数类 F1 从 0.683 升至 0.811；量化检测器适配片上存储，星上推理吞吐达 25-30 帧/秒。

Conclusion: 实验验证该工作流可作为实时自主空中监视的决策支持工具，有效突破传统被动采集架构的瓶颈，实现纳卫星星上智能处理与稀有目标鲁棒检测。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [175] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种模糊规则神经符号框架用于下水道管道严重程度评估，结合Swin Transformer预测14种检查CODE等级与从J48决策树提取的19条IF-THEN规则，通过模糊逻辑推理实现可解释的严重程度分类。在3,244张图像（5个高度不平衡严重等级）上，相比纯图像分类，准确率提升17.9%、平衡准确率提升12.2%、Macro F1提升23.0%、MCC提升17.3%。真值标签由5个大语言模型对检查员记录共识生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化下水道管道严重程度评估依赖直接图像分类，形成“黑箱”，视觉缺陷与最终严重程度评分之间的联系隐性化，缺乏可解释性和可追溯性，需要将神经感知与符号推理解耦。

Method: 模块化模糊规则神经符号框架：(1) 感知模块：Swin Transformer从图像直接预测14种多标签检查CODE等级；(2) 推理模块：在真值CODE与严重程度标签上训练J48(C4.5)决策树，将路径转换为19条固定IF-THEN规则；(3) 推理采用模糊逻辑：CODE条件的t-norm激活经规则置信度加权，通过对应s-norm组合产生可解释的类别证据。对比Product、Łukasiewicz、Hamacher算子对。数据集3,244图像跨5个高度不平衡严重等级，真值标签由5个独立LLM对原始检查员记录共识生成。

Result: 与纯图像分类相比，准确率提升17.9%、平衡准确率提升12.2%、Macro F1提升23.0%、MCC提升17.3%。框架在类别平衡性能上具有竞争力，且实现了从预测CODE等级到规则支持再到严重程度证据的可追溯推理。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架成功将神经感知与符号推理解耦，为不平衡下水道检查数据提供了可解释、可追溯的严重程度评估，在提升分类指标的同时保持了推理路径的透明性。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [176] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SCOPE框架，通过令牌化供应链实体、共享运营表示和系统级效用评估，实现端到端补货计划的联合优化，在真实数据上优于分阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链中品类选择、来源分配、补货频次和路由决策虽强耦合，却常由不同部门和系统分别优化，导致缺货、库存风险和运输浪费，亟需统一的运营规划方法。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination），将供应链实体建模为令牌，通过共享运营表示进行上下文化，映射至对应决策接口；各决策在前序决策形成的部分计划基础上构建，完整计划由共享系统级效用评估，应用于城市生鲜零售补货场景。

Result: 在叮咚买菜和京东两大规模供应链的真实运营数据上实验，SCOPE在两种补货层级上均显著优于分阶段优化基线和实务导向基线，验证了跨部门运营耦合的学习与协调能提升端到端决策效果。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模和协调供应链多决策耦合，SCOPE实现了更有效的端到端补货规划，证明了统一运营规划优于孤立决策模块的优势。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [177] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个动态更新的基准测试（InfoOpsBench），用于评估前沿语言模型抵抗国家支持的信息操作的能力。测试了来自8个供应商的17个模型，发现拒绝率（完整性得分）在8.8%到94.5%之间差异巨大，且与模型大小无关。模型行为差异显著：有的会捏造细节、产生更有害输出，有的则会在遵从中化解声明，事实核查率从2.9%到72.9%不等。中国开发的模型（除Z.ai的GLM 5.2外）在涉及中国批评的事实性声明上大幅降低遵从率（下降48-70个百分点），显示出选择性拒绝。基准每周更新，抗饱和性强，揭示了模型可用性与安全性之间的平衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态基准测试容易饱和，无法持续评估模型抵抗实时演变的国家支持信息操作的能力。作者旨在构建一个持续更新的活基准，利用实时监控管道追踪俄、中、伊三国国家支持的信息资产，积累了2100多个信息操作案例，以动态衡量模型完整性。

Method: 从实时监控管道收集2100+个国家支持的信息操作案例，构建动态基准并配套每周更新的网站（pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench）。测试17个模型（8个供应商），采用四种提示框架，测量拒绝率（完整性得分），并分析模型输出的特征：是否捏造细节、是否化解声明、事实核查率等。同时设计匹配的良性声明对照组，考察模型在涉及中国批评声明上的选择性拒绝行为。

Result: 完整性得分（拒绝率）跨度85.7个百分点（8.8%-94.5%），不能用模型规模解释。模型选择改变操作性质：部分模型捏造细节产生更有害输出，部分在遵从中化解声明，事实核查率差异大（2.9%-72.9%）。完整性与对良性声明的拒绝相关，凸显可用性与安全性的权衡。中国开发模型（除GLM 5.2外）在涉及中国批评的事实性声明上遵从率大幅下降48-70个百分点，显示出地缘政治敏感性导致的选择性合规。

Conclusion: 动态基准InfoOpsBench有效抵抗饱和，持续追踪真实世界信息操作。评估揭示模型在抵抗国家支持信息操作上存在巨大差异，且行为模式多样。中国模型表现出明显的地缘政治选择性拒绝，突显了在模型安全对齐中平衡通用性与特定敏感话题处理的挑战。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [178] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度限制信念更新框架，允许根据源世界将复合认知输入转化为较弱的代理输入，再进行可信度限制转换。提供了语义和公理化刻画，识别出一致性保持和最大一致性保持两个子类，并证明该框架涵盖现有方法且表达力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可信度限制信念更新将认知输入视为不可分整体，无法表示复合认知输入仅部分可实现的情况。需要一种机制允许根据源世界选择性接受输入的部分内容。

Method: 引入选择性可信度限制信念更新：对每个源世界，通过转换函数将认知输入映射为较弱的代理输入，再应用可信度限制转换。给出语义刻画（基于可能世界的可达关系和转换函数）和公理化刻画（一组后验公理），并定义一致性保持和最大一致性保持两个子类。

Result: 证明了选择性可信度限制更新算子的语义与公理等价；一致性保持算子要求原输入一致时转换后的输入可信；最大一致性保持算子进一步要求代理输入在可信后果中信息量最大。该框架以可信度限制更新为特例，去除可信度限制且转换函数为恒等时退化为Katsuno-Mendelzon更新，表达力严格更强。

Conclusion: 选择性可信度限制信念更新提供了统一且更具表达力的框架，支持源世界依赖的选择性接受，兼容并扩展了现有信念更新方法。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [179] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MANTA 框架，使大语言模型多智能体系统的通信拓扑能在推理时自进化，解决了现有系统将拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标的局限。实验表明 MANTA 在五大基准上平均得分 74.0，超越最强基线 5.8 个百分点，证明推理时的自我改进可延伸至协作架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计选择或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理阶段根据任务动态调整协作结构的能力，限制了复杂问题求解的灵活性和效率。

Method: MANTA 框架包含两阶段：执行前根据任务条件从先验结构经验初始化拓扑；部署期间监控协作轨迹，在当前组织不足时施加有界结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链路、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理五个基准上评测，MANTA 以 74.0 的平均分位居第一，比最强基线高出 5.8 个百分点，并在 PlanCraft 上取得最佳成绩。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进机制可以扩展到多智能体协作架构层面，动态自适应的通信拓扑能显著提升复杂任务求解性能。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [180] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在硬件受限环境下，推理时计算扩展对本地计算机使用代理（CUA）性能的影响。实验表明，增加计算往往带来边际收益递减并改变失败模式：上下文扩展虽能提升轨迹稳定性和任务准确性，但收益随Token成本增加而饱和，失败模式从重复/停滞转向过早虚假成功；时间扩展虽减少最大步数停滞，但不显著提升任务成功率，表明更长视野常延续错误轨迹而非修正；结构分解在两阶段代理中引入规划与格式开销；并行扩展虽能部分缓解这些失败，但计算成本高昂。结论指出高效本地CUA需选择性计算分配、感知失败的控制机制，以及适配本地模型能力局限的代理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着隐私、成本效率和实用性需求增长，本地部署自主计算机使用代理（CUA）愈发重要，但严格硬件约束下提升其性能仍具挑战。现有研究显示推理时扩展可改善前沿CUA，但其对资源受限本地模型的效果尚不明确。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上评估Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B，从上下文、时间、结构、并行四个维度系统实证研究推理时扩展对本地CUA的影响。

Result: 上下文扩展提供历史依据，改善轨迹稳定性和任务准确性，但收益随Token成本增加饱和，失败模式从重复/停滞转向过早虚假成功；时间扩展减少最大步数停滞，却不显著提升任务成功率，表明更长视野常延续错误轨迹；结构分解在两阶段本地代理中引入规划与格式开销；并行扩展虽部分缓解失败，但计算成本高昂。整体呈现边际收益递减且失败模式转移。

Conclusion: 高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、感知失败的控制机制，以及围绕本地模型能力与局限设计的代理框架。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [181] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DualG-MRAG，一种双层图增强的多模态检索增强生成框架，通过宏观推理图和微观匹配图解耦全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配，利用GNN检索器实现查询驱动的消息传递，并引入动态规划解码机制从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，显著提升复杂多跳推理任务的证据召回率和问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG方法在复杂多跳推理任务上表现不佳：实例级独立匹配无法捕捉跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法面临粒度困境——细粒度视觉特征导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，粗粒度表示则丢弃关键局部证据。

Method: 提出DualG-MRAG双层解耦架构：1) 宏观图用于全局拓扑路由，微观图用于精确局部验证，隔离全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配以抑制检索噪声；2) 将检索建模为查询驱动的消息传递过程，通过GNN检索器实现异构证据源间的动态相关性传播；3) 引入动态规划解码机制，从GNN前向传播中直接提取显式推理路径，替代传统孤立文档块输入，为生成模型提供连贯结构指导。

Result: 大量实验表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确率上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DualG-MRAG通过宏微观图解耦、GNN检索器动态消息传递及动态规划解码提取推理路径，有效解决了多模态RAG在复杂多跳推理中的粒度困境和结构建模不足问题，为多模态检索增强生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 BridgeMIL，一种两阶段框架，解决 EEG 疾病诊断中实例级标签继承不可靠且主体级样本稀缺的问题。第一阶段通过时序邻近窗口对齐和主体内子包采样进行无监督预训练，利用方差和协方差正则化防止崩溃和冗余；第二阶段将编码器迁移至注意力机制的 MIL 聚合器，仅在主体级预测上施加监督，并通过特征保持限制表示漂移。在三个 EEG 疾病数据集和五种骨干网络上，BridgeMIL 在 15 个数据集-骨干组合中有 14 个达到最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率 76.57%，比最强基线高 4.28 个百分点。进一步分析揭示继承标签可靠性差异大，性能对主体稀缺比实例稀缺更敏感，表示空间形成清晰的主体级聚类且类别分离度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 疾病诊断流程通常将长程记录切分为短实例，继承主体标签给每个实例，训练实例级分类器，隐含假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据。多实例学习 (MIL) 虽避免继承标签，但 EEG 数据集主体数远少于实例数，导致端到端 MIL 学到的表示质量受限。因此需要一种方法解耦实例表示学习与主体级监督，利用丰富的实例数据而不给单个实例分配疾病标签。

Method: BridgeMIL 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段预训练编码器，不使用继承的实例标签，通过对齐时序邻近窗口和独立采样的主体内子包进行自监督学习，引入方差正则化防止表示崩溃、协方差正则化降低冗余，无需负样本对；第二阶段将预训练编码器迁移到基于注意力的 MIL 聚合器，仅在主体级预测上施加监督，并通过特征保持机制限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个 EEG 疾病数据集和五种代表性骨干网络上，BridgeMIL 在 15 个数据集-骨干组合中有 14 个获得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率 76.57%，比最强基线提升 4.28 个百分点。分析显示：继承标签的可靠性在实例间差异显著；模型性能对主体数量稀缺的敏感度高于对实例数量稀缺；学到的表示空间呈现清晰的主体级聚类，且诊断类别间分离度更好。

Conclusion: 研究强调了将监督信号与主体级预测目标对齐的重要性，同时利用大量 EEG 实例数据而不给单个实例分配疾病标签。BridgeMIL 通过解耦表示学习与主体级监督，有效缓解了标签继承噪声和主体稀缺问题，为 EEG 疾病诊断提供了更可靠的范式。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [183] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文测试了4位量化对多轮工具调用代理的影响，发现标准指标上无显著差异，但量化放大了现有失败（工具名称幻觉），错误量增加2.5倍，但未引入新错误。基准的错误预算掩盖了这一影响，缩减预算可揭示17分差距。针对性提示修复可消除损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证后训练量化到4位权重是否对多轮工具调用代理近乎无损的声称，因为在该场景下量化影响最为关键。

Method: 在τ^2-bench上，跨两个开放权重模型家族（稠密和MoE变体）及两个领域（八个单元格，每个456回合），在16、8、4位权重下评估。使用标准指标、多重比较校正、等价性检验、错误量分析、秩相关、错误预算缩减实验、针对性错误修复提示实验。

Result: 标准指标上量化无显著得分变化（等价性检验界限±7.5分）。但量化放大了现有失败：电信领域工具名称幻觉错误量增加2.5倍（+17.6分/任务），零售实体错误同向趋势。失败集合在各精度下相同（秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。十错误预算掩盖了额外失败。将预算缩至两错误揭示17分差距，仅在量化增加错误量的单元格出现。针对性错误修复提示在五个电信模型上各精度下精准消除损害。

Conclusion: 量化并非无损，它放大了现有系统性错误而非引入新错误。标准基准指标因错误预算机制掩盖了这一过程损害。建议报告每通道错误率和收缩预算下的成功率等诊断指标，以揭示量化的真实影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [184] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“长时程失败”的严格定义，主张通过对比完整任务成功率与短阶段基线预测（即“时程残差”）来量化长时程退化，并强调需控制实验配置以隔离上下文腐烂等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程基准虽观察到任务越长失败率越高，但未区分普通错误累积与轨迹诱导退化（如前序执行使后续决策更难、上下文腐烂），缺乏量化指标来厘清失败根因。

Method: 定义“时程残差”为完整任务成功率与基于短阶段独立成功率预测值的对数比率；要求基准实验使用相同智能体配置，预先指定阶段划分、检查点、信息可见性和预算分配。

Result: 时程残差能揭示完整滚动与基线预测的偏离程度，但无法直接解释退化原因（如上下文腐烂、环境变化等），仍需针对性实验进一步诊断。

Conclusion: 规范化的长时程评估应采用时程残差作为核心指标，并结合可控消融实验，才能将“任务越长越失败”细化为可归因的具体机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [185] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE 是一种基于风险引导的子空间混合专家模型，通过样本级模态可靠性估计和专家子空间兼容性实现稀疏路由，在四个生物医学多模态数据集上显著优于现有方法，同时具备良好的概率校准和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法虽通过跨模态交互和自适应融合提升性能，但融合权重无法揭示模态是否不可靠、冗余或与专家不匹配，导致融合决策缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 TIER-MoE：1) 以样本级单模态预测损失作为模态可靠性风险，利用 out-of-fold 预测学习该风险；2) 将风险与专家特定子空间兼容性结合，实现稀疏模态-专家路由；3) 保留始终激活的共享路径以维持多模态互补性。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病、皮肤病变恶性程度、视网膜分类四个公开数据集上，TIER-MoE 在 Macro-F1 和 Brier score 上持续优于 SOTA 方法，概率校准显著改善，且在外部队列上表现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: TIER-MoE 通过风险引导的子空间 MoE 架构，有效解决了模态可靠性评估与专家匹配的问题，实现了更鲁棒、可校准且泛化性强的多模态融合。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [186] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCause框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）根据专家标签提出语义合理的因果图编辑，结合确定性代码验证节点身份和无环性，在标注对齐集上保留最佳图，实现根因分析的透明预测。同时发布TeleRCA基准。实验显示EvoCause优于未经细化的PC基线，且警报名称信息对图细化有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法学习固定的因果图，无法利用历史事件的专家诊断；专家标签仅约束源节点，未指定具体边编辑，需要一种方法闭环专家知识与因果图学习。

Method: EvoCause框架：利用LLM提出语义合理的图编辑，确定性代码验证节点身份和无环性，在标注对齐集上选择最佳图；测试时仅使用细化后的图进行透明预测，无需LLM调用。同时发布TeleRCA专家标注基准。

Result: 合成数据上，EvoCause初始化PC算法优于未细化PC基线，Node F1提升11.59pp，Case EM提升9.40pp，Graph F1提升4.59pp，nSHD降低0.2379。TeleRCA上，将可读警报标题替换为匿名标识符导致Node F1和Case EM分别下降6.12pp和8.04pp，表明警报名称信息有助于图细化。

Conclusion: EvoCause通过LLM辅助的图编辑有效整合专家知识，提升根因分析性能；TeleRCA基准为真实电信网络告警根因分析提供评测标准；警报语义信息对因果图细化至关重要。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [187] [THGFM: Dual-Branch Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27303)
*Yixin Peng,Diego Collarana,Er Jin,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出THGFM，一种大规模时序异构图融合模型，通过双路径架构（共享空间时序注意力和关系类型分区时序注意力）结合类型条件非竞争门控求和融合，并引入旋转时序注意力将相对时间直接编码进注意力分数，在多个学术图基准上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序异构图学习方法难以同时实现参数高效的跨类型迁移与关系感知的专业化建模，且通常仅将时间作为注意力核外部的加性特征注入，限制了时序信息的深度融合。

Method: THGFM采用双路径架构：1) 共享空间时序注意力分支实现参数高效的跨类型迁移；2) 关系类型分区时序注意力分支实现关系感知的专业化建模。两者通过双路径关系-共享融合模块集成，采用类型条件非竞争门控求和融合机制，为共享分支和专业化分支分配独立的、类型条件的逐特征门控，允许两者同时被放大或抑制而非零和竞争。此外引入旋转时序注意力，通过半相位旋转查询和键向量来直接将相对时间信息编码进注意力分数计算中。

Result: 在学术图基准上，THGFM在六个任务上的平均性能提升3.25%，峰值相对增益分别为：OAG-CS PV +12.37%，PF-L2 +4.87%，PF-L1 +1.18%，OGBN-MAG +4.24%，HTAG-ArXiv +3.73%，HTAG-DBLP +4.61%。

Conclusion: THGFM通过创新的双路径融合架构和旋转时序注意力机制，有效解决了时序异构图学习中跨类型迁移与关系专业化难以兼得、时间信息融合不充分的问题，在多个大规模基准上取得了显著的性能提升，为大规模时序异构图建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Temporal heterogeneous graphs offer a natural abstraction for dynamic relational systems in which diverse node and relation types co-exist and evolve over time. Learning on such graphs requires jointly modeling cross-type structural heterogeneity and the temporal dynamics of interactions, yet existing methods still struggle to reconcile parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with relation-aware specialization, and typically inject time only as additive features outside the attention kernel. We propose \textbf{THGFM}, a web-scale temporal heterogeneous graph fusion model that addresses both limitations within a unified dual-path architecture. THGFM couples a \textit{Shared-Space Temporal Attention} branch for parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with a \textit{Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention} branch for relation-aware specialization, and integrates them through \textit{Dual-Path Relational--Shared Fusion}, instantiated with \textit{Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion}: a adaptive mechanism that assigns independent, type-conditioned feature-wise gates to the shared and specialized branches, allowing both to be amplified or suppressed without zero-sum competition. To directly incorporate relative time into the attention score, THGFM further introduces \textit{Rotary Temporal Attention}, which rotates queries and keys by half-phases of relative time before matching. THGFM consistently outperforms baseline graph transformer models on academic graphs benchmarks, delivering a $+3.25\%$ six-task mean gain, with peak relative gains of $+12.37\%$ on OAG-CS PV, $+4.87\%$ on PF-$L_2$, and $+1.18\%$ on PF-$L_1$, and $+4.24\%$, $+3.73\%$, and $+4.61\%$ on OGBN-MAG, HTAG-ArXiv, and HTAG-DBLP, respectively.

</details>


### [188] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal,Subhrajyoti Basu,Aritra Gir Mahanta,Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Mediate框架，通过扰动医学视觉语言模型生成的推理中的临床属性来测量预测是否跟随编辑后的推理，发现推理注入机制显著影响忠实度测量，上下文位置而非声明来源是决定模型是否使用推理的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答临床问题前生成思维链推理，但不清楚该推理是否因果性地影响预测，需要一种方法来评估推理对预测的中介作用。

Method: 提出CoT-Mediate框架，采用双臂协议比较重新提示证据与前缀强制延续，结合溯源控制干预仅改变相同推理的归属来源，以区分推理中介与迎合行为。在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上各评估1000个VQA-RAD样本。

Result: 前缀强制延续的中介忠实度一致高于重新提示；溯源分析揭示模型特定的遵从行为差异。移除视觉证据会增加对注入推理的依赖，而侧别性是最不忠实跟踪的临床属性。

Conclusion: 推理注入机制显著影响测量的忠实度，上下文位置而非声明的溯源是决定医学视觉语言模型是否使用其生成推理的主要决定因素。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [189] [ROCS: Request-Oriented Compute Sharing for Efficient Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27744)
*Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Jian Jiao,Boda Li,Haoyu Wang,Tongyi Tang,Ao Cai,Zijian Shen,Zhengkai Zhang,Wenyi Xie,Ryan Dick,Han Liu,Neng Shi,Bin Yu,Jianbo Xiao,Shuyao Bi,Hongtao Yu,Yuanwei Fang,Zhuoran Zhao,Sijia Chen,Yang Chen,Shuqi Yang,Qianru Li,Zikun Liu,Wei Ling,Sihan Zeng,Longhao Jin,Jiaxin Lu,Yinbin Ma,Jiawei Li,Yichen Ruan,Yong Ler Lee,Birmingham Guan,Zijian Li,Jianbo Sun,Zhengyu Zhang,Zeliang Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yuchen Hao,GP Musumeci,Venkatesh Ranganathan,Yantao Yao,Chunqiang Tang,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Ellie Dingqiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROCS（面向请求的计算共享）范式，通过将请求侧特征计算在候选项间共享，配合GLM、DCA和IKBO三大技术，在不降低预测质量前提下实现高达3倍的QPS提升，已在大规模广告与推荐系统落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型通过扩大特征交互与序列模块提升预测质量，但生产环境成本约束限制了进一步扩展。推理时每个用户请求需对多个候选项评分，而请求侧特征在候选间共享，现有系统却重复计算请求侧表示，造成巨大算力浪费。

Method: 1) ROCS范式：将请求-候选交互推迟到模型后段，隔离候选相关表示，使请求侧计算仅执行一次而非每候选一次。2) GLM（广义层掩码）：在特征交互架构中强制候选隔离。3) DCA（深度交叉注意力）：将请求导向共享扩展到序列架构。4) IKBO（核内广播优化）：为GPU部署协同设计的加速内核。

Result: 公开基准上ROCS在多种骨干网络上一致改善质量-效率权衡。生产负载：检索模型QPS提升3倍且无质量损失；短视频排序模型相对LogLoss降低0.5%且QPS增50%。已跨广告/自然流、检索/排序、推理复杂度跨两个数量级的系统部署，显著降低基建成本。

Conclusion: ROCS通过将请求侧计算前置共享、候选侧计算后置隔离，配合算子级GPU优化，在保持甚至提升预测质量的同时大幅降低推理延迟与算力成本，证明了请求导向计算共享在工业级推荐系统中的普适性与高价值。

Abstract: Modern recommendation models gain prediction quality by scaling feature-interaction and sequence modules, but production cost constraints cap how far systems can scale.
  In this work, we propose Request-Oriented Compute Sharing (ROCS), a modeling and inference paradigm that exploits a unique property of recommendation inference: each user request is evaluated against many candidates, while request-side features are shared across candidates. ROCS defers request-candidate interactions as late as possible, isolates candidate-dependent representations, and evaluates substantial portions of the model once per request rather than once per candidate, significantly improving inference efficiency while maintaining or improving prediction quality. To realize this paradigm, we develop Generalized Layer Masking (GLM) to enforce candidate isolation in feature-interaction architectures, and Deep Cross Attention (DCA) to extend request-oriented sharing to sequence architectures. To support efficient GPU deployment, we co-design In-Kernel Broadcast Optimization (IKBO) that significantly accelerates ROCS model execution.
  Experiments on public benchmarks show that ROCS consistently improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff across recommendation backbones. On production-scale workloads, ROCS achieves up to a 3x QPS improvement on retrieval models without quality degradation and a 0.5% relative LogLoss improvement with a 50% QPS gain on a short-form video ranking model. ROCS has been deployed across large-scale recommendation systems spanning ads and organic surfaces, retrieval and ranking stages, and more than two orders of magnitude in inference complexity, delivering significant online gains at reduced infrastructure cost.

</details>


### [190] [ZUNA1.1: A more flexible EEG foundation model for Denoising and Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27308)
*Christopher Warner,Jonas Mago,JR Huml,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZUNA1.1 是一个 3.8 亿参数的扩散自编码器，用于灵活的 EEG 信号重建，支持变长序列（最长 30 秒）、任意通道数与头皮位置、任意时间段的重建，性能至少持平前代 ZUNA1，且显著优于球面样条插值等标准方法，已开源（Apache 2.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 重建方法（如球面样条插值）灵活性有限，难以处理变长序列、任意通道布局和局部时间段重建等多样化任务，需一种统一、灵活且高性能的重建模型。

Method: 采用扩散自编码器架构，参数量 3.8 亿，设计为可处理最长 30 秒的变长 EEG 序列，支持任意数量和位置的通道，并能重建通道内的任意时间区间以及整个通道。

Result: ZUNA1.1 性能至少与 ZUNA1 持平，但灵活性大幅提升，可适应广泛的重建任务；在标准基准上显著优于 MNE 包中普遍部署的球面样条插值等传统去噪和重建方法。

Conclusion: ZUNA1.1 为 EEG 信号重建提供了一个统一、灵活且高性能的开源解决方案，填补了现有方法在处理变长、任意通道布局和局部时间段重建方面的空白，有望推动 EEG 分析的实际应用。

Abstract: We introduce ZUNA1.1, a 380M-parameter diffusion autoencoder for flexible EEG signal reconstruction. ZUNA1.1 is capable of reconstructing variable length sequences of up to 30s, with an arbitrary number of EEG channels at arbitrary scalp locations, and can reconstruct arbitrary temporal intervals within channels in addition to reconstructing entire channels. We demonstrate that ZUNA1.1 performs at least on par with our earlier ZUNA1 model, while being far more flexible and capable of handling a wide range of reconstruction tasks. ZUNA1.1 continues to substantially outperform standard EEG denoising and reconstruction methods such as spherical spline interpolation, which is ubiquitously deployed in the MNE package. The ZUNA1.1 model is released open source under the permissive Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [191] [Modeling Decisions in Blockchain Analytics: A Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Tree-Based vs. Sequential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27350)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对以太坊女巫机器人检测问题，指出现有深度学习序列模型存在计算开销大、标签泄露导致性能虚高等问题。提出Blind-Spot协议消除高信号合约捷径，设计交易语法表示捕捉钱包行为的节奏、EVM执行结构与意图。在防泄露评估下，XGBoost树模型优于Transformer/BiLSTM序列模型，且具备更低延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有女巫检测方法多采用深度学习将链上活动视为准语言序列，但复杂序列模型计算昂贵难以实时监控，且报告性能可能因高信号智能合约带来的标签泄露而虚高。需研究：有机用户、女巫机器人、MEV机器人在交易历史结构复杂度上是否存在差异；消除泄露后序列模型是否仍优于表格树模型；交易顺序还是时序提供更强行为信号；生成模型是否适合低延迟部署。

Method: 提出防泄露女巫检测框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) Blind-Spot协议：通过屏蔽高信号合约交易消除捷径特征；(2) 交易语法表示：从节奏、EVM执行结构、意图三个维度建模钱包行为。实验对比Transformer、BiLSTM序列模型与XGBoost、SVM基线模型，在以太坊行分类任务上进行防泄露评估。

Result: 在防泄露评估条件下，XGBoost树模型在分类性能上超越Transformer和BiLSTM等序列模型，同时具备显著更低的推理延迟和预估能耗。交易语法表示有效捕捉行为差异，Blind-Spot协议成功消除标签泄露带来的虚高性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对以太坊行分类任务，结合防泄露协议与结构化交易语法表示的传统树模型（XGBoost）优于复杂深度序列模型，且更适合低延迟、低能耗的实际部署。这为区块链实体检测提供了实用、高效的范式。

Abstract: Sybil bots are Ethereum actors that imitate legitimate users to extract airdrop rewards or influence governance. Recent Sybil detection methods increasingly use deep learning and treat blockchain activity as a quasi-linguistic sequence. However, complex sequence models are computationally expensive for real-time monitoring, and their reported performance may be inflated by label leakage from high-signal smart contracts. We ask whether and how organic users, Sybil bots, and MEV bots differ in the structural complexity of their transaction histories; whether sequential models outperform tree-based tabular models once leakage is reduced; whether transaction order or timing provides the stronger behavioral signal; and whether the resulting models are practical for low-latency deployment. Our approach to leakage-aware Sybil bot detection consists of a Blind-Spot protocol and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. The former eliminates shortcuts associated with high-signal contracts, whereas the latter models wallets using rhythm, EVM execution structure, and intent. We evaluate this approach on Ethereum actor classification by comparing Transformer and BiLSTM sequence models against XGBoost and SVM baselines. We contribute a framework for leakage-aware Ethereum actor classification and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. Our results demonstrate that, under leakage-aware evaluation, XGBoost outperforms Transformer-based sequence models while providing lower latency and estimated energy use.

</details>


### [192] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压缩的相似度（NCD）方法，利用EVM执行轨迹构建符号化交易语法，通过盲点协议过滤高信号合约，构建行为图以发现Sybil攻击者，无需监督训练且不依赖显性资金链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sybil检测方法依赖代币转账图构建或监督学习闭集分类，易受攻击策略变化和规避战术影响，且需要显性金融链接。需要一种无监督、抗时序漂移和对抗扰动、不依赖资金链接的检测方法。

Method: 从EVM追踪中提取符号化交易语法，捕捉交易节奏、执行结构、功能意图；设计盲点协议过滤高信号合约；使用Gzip-based NCD构建行为图；在种子钱包基础上扩展可疑钱包；验证包括监督学习基线、时序分割、合成伪装压力测试。

Result: 该框架在Sybil候选发现上优于监督基线，对时序漂移和对抗扰动具有鲁棒性，能在无显性资金链接情况下扩展可疑种子钱包，作为无训练局部发现原语适用于开放世界区块链审计。

Conclusion: 提出了一种泄露感知行为框架，核心NCD原语无需监督训练，可作为开放世界区块链审计的无训练局部发现原语，而非正式开放集识别系统。

Abstract: Sybil attackers are Blockchain actors that adopt the characteristics of regular users to exploit airdrops or influence governance. Current methods of Sybil actor detection include constructing graphs, which requires token transfers between examined wallets. Machine learning algorithms have been employed as well, but they treat the task as a closed-set classification problem, making them vulnerable to frequent changes in attack strategies or evasion tactics. We address the following questions: can compression-based similarity differentiate Sybil bots, organic users, and arbitrage bot wallets without direct financial links? What is the effect of high-signal contracts on the discovery of Sybils, and how robust are behavioral graphs under temporal drift and adversarial perturbations? Our approach synthesizes a symbolic Transaction Grammar from EVM (Ethereum Virtual Machine) traces, capturing separately transaction rhythm, execution structure, and functional intent. The high-signal contracts are filtered with our own protocol, called the Blind-Spot Protocol. Gzip-based NCD is used to construct a behavioral graph for Sybil discovery. We validate this framework against supervised machine learning baselines, a temporal split, and synthetic camouflage stress tests. Ultimately, we contribute a leakage-aware behavioral framework for Sybil candidate discovery. Its core NCD primitive requires no supervised training and can expand suspicious seed wallets without explicit funding links. We position the method as a training-free local discovery primitive for open-world blockchain audits, rather than as a formal open-set recognition system.

</details>


### [193] [Explorative Modeling: Unlocking a Third Pretraining Axis and End-to-End Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27372)
*Alexi Gladstone,Heng Ji,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出探索式建模（Explorative Modeling, XMs）这一新范式，通过在训练循环中探索K个候选匹配并训练最优匹配，而非分解生成过程，实现了端到端生成建模。探索成为继参数量和数据量之后的第三个预训练轴，随规模单调提升性能，并在图像、视频、语言等连续离散域上显著提升算力、样本、参数效率，同时以16-256倍更少推理步数匹配扩散模型控制任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模的核心是处理多模态分布，现有可扩展方法均通过分解生成过程来处理，这阻碍了端到端训练。AlexNet开启的深度学习革命证明端到端训练优于手工分解阶段，但生成建模一直是个例外。本文旨在打破这一限制，实现真正的端到端生成建模。

Method: 探索式建模（XMs）：不再分解生成过程，而是分解训练循环。在训练时探索K个模型生成与数据之间的候选匹配，仅在最佳匹配上计算损失并更新模型，使预测聚焦于特定模式而非模糊平均。

Result: 1) 探索成新预训练轴：随探索规模单调提升性能，增益随数据规模从7%升至36%，随模型规模从13%升至23%；3倍算力下效率增益翻倍。2) 效率大幅提升：FLOP效率4.1倍、样本效率6.2倍、参数效率47%。3) 图像生成SOTA：无引导下ImageNet FID达1.43。4) 端到端重建生成：控制任务以16-256倍更少推理步匹配扩散模型。

Conclusion: 探索式模型（XMs）确立了现有生成模型的新预训练轴，同时作为独立的端到端生成建模范式，解决了生成建模长期无法端到端训练的根本问题，在规模化、效率、泛化与推理速度上均实现突破。

Abstract: The deep learning revolution, kicked off by AlexNet, taught us that end-to-end training beats decomposing a problem into hand-designed stages. Generative modeling, however, has remained the exception-despite generative models being remarkably capable, they are still not trained end-to-end. This is because, at its core, generative modeling is about handling distributions with many modes, and existing scalable approaches handle this the same way, by factoring the generation procedure, which prevents end-to-end generation. In this work, we introduce Explorative Modeling, a new paradigm that instead factors the training loop, exploring K candidate matches between model generations and data, and training on the best, so predictions commit to modes rather than blurring them. We find Explorative Models (XMs) useful in two settings. First, increasing exploration adds a third pretraining axis beyond parameters and data for existing generative models-where scaling exploration monotonically improves performance across both continuous and discrete domains (images, video, and language). Notably, gains from exploration increase with scale, climbing from 7% to 36% as data scales and from 13% to 23% as models grow, with efficiency gains more than doubling at 3x the compute. Concretely, exploration improves FLOP efficiency by 4.1x, sample efficiency by 6.2x, parameter efficiency by 47%, lifts the strongest of image-generation recipes to a near-state-of-the-art 1.43 FID on ImageNet without guidance, enables scaling how end-to-end existing models are, and unlocks scaling generalization. Second, XMs enable end-to-end reconstructive generative modeling, matching diffusion on control tasks with 16-256x fewer inference steps. Together, these results establish XMs as both a new pretraining axis for existing generative models and a standalone end-to-end generative modeling paradigm.

</details>


### [194] [The Convergence Behavior of Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27383)
*Yijiang Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为原始向量形式的 Adam 优化器在重尾随机噪声下建立了收敛保证，证明其收敛到 (ρ,ε)-平稳点，但迭代复杂度次优且依赖于 p；若已知域半径并用于控制在线学习器输出，则可达到最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Adam 变体在有界方差非凸优化中已知达到最优迭代复杂度，但对于仅具有有界 p 阶中心矩 (p∈(1,2]) 的随机梯度（这一设定在现代深度学习中日益常见）的行为知之甚少，因此需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 推广近期的在线到非凸转换框架以适应重尾马鞭差分噪声，并基于该广义框架开发了 Adam 的折扣遗憾分析，不需要限制性的参数耦合。

Result: Adam 在重尾噪声下收敛到 (ρ,ε)-平稳点，但表现出次优迭代复杂度和 p 依赖的收敛，这种次优性即使在有界方差情况 (p=2) 下依然存在；当域半径已知并用于控制在线学习器输出时（相关文献中的标准设置），收敛速率提升至匹配最优复杂度。

Conclusion: 这些发现为 Adam 在重尾噪声制度下的鲁棒性和局限性提供了新的理论洞见。

Abstract: We establish the first convergence guarantees for the plain vector-form \emph{Adam} optimizer under heavy-tailed stochastic noise. While several Adam variants are known to achieve optimal iteration complexity in bounded-variance nonconvex optimization, little is understood about their behavior when stochastic gradients admit only a bounded $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1,2]$, a setting increasingly observed in modern deep learning. To address this gap, we generalize the recent online-to-nonconvex conversion framework to accommodate heavy-tailed martingale-difference noise. Building on this generalized framework, we develop a discounted regret analysis for Adam, without restrictive parameter coupling. Our results show that Adam converges to $(ρ,ε)$-stationary points under heavy-tailed noise. However, it exhibits a suboptimal iteration complexity and $p$-dependent convergence, a suboptimality that persists even in the bounded-variance case ($p=2$). When the domain radius is known and used to control the online-learner output, a standard setup in related literature, the convergence rate improves to match the optimal complexity. These findings provide new theoretical insight into the robustness and limitations of Adam in heavy-tailed regimes.

</details>


### [195] [FunL2O: LLM-Guided Feature Function Design for Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27389)
*Bingheng Li,Junyang Cai,Yupeng Zhang,Bistra Dilkina,Jayant Kalagnanam,Dzung T. Phan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FunL2O，首个基于 LLM 驱动程序进化的统一框架，自动化 Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) 中的特征设计，在连续与离散优化任务上均超越手工特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 L2O 方法依赖手工设计的特征函数，表示设计过程人工且固化，难以跨领域泛化，缺乏自动化特征工程机制。

Method: 采用 FunSearch 风格的进化循环：LLM 生成可执行的特征函数代码，固定评估流程重新训练原 L2O 模型并测量下游优化性能，迭代筛选高性能特征。

Result: 在线性/二次规划（解预测、热启动）及混合整数优化（GNN 引导后门分支、Predict-and-Search）任务上，跨四种 LLM 的进化特征一致优于手工特征。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的特征进化是一种通用且有效的 L2O 表示设计自动化方法，显著降低了特征工程的人工成本并提升了求解性能。

Abstract: Learning-to-optimize (L2O) methods accelerate repeated optimization by training models to predict solutions, warm starts, branching decisions, or other forms of solver guidance. A critical yet largely overlooked component of these pipelines is the feature function that maps problem instances to inputs for machine learning models. Existing L2O methods typically rely on hand-crafted features, making representation design manual and largely fixed across domains. We introduce FunL2O, the first unified framework for automating feature design through LLM-driven program evolution for L2O. In a FunSearch-style loop, an LLM proposes executable feature functions, while a fixed evaluation process retrains the original L2O model and measures downstream optimization performance. We evaluate FunL2O on linear and quadratic programming tasks involving solution prediction and warm-starting, as well as on mixed-integer optimization tasks using GNN-guided backdoor branching and Predict-and-Search. Across continuous and discrete optimization tasks and four LLMs, the evolved features consistently outperform hand-crafted representations. These results establish LLM-driven feature evolution as a general and effective approach to automating representation design in L2O.

</details>


### [196] [ECG-InterpBench: Benchmarking the Interpretability of ECG Foundation Models with Matched-Scale Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27404)
*Yixuan Duan,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ECG-InterpBench，一个系统评估心电图基础模型表示可解释性的基准，使用稀疏自编码器作为标准化测量工具，跨模型匹配容量，对六个冻结模型在五种编码器深度、五种匹配字典宽度、三个随机种子上进行评估，生成 450 单元可解释性图谱，揭示模型间存在不同的可解释性画像，且重建保真度与临床可访问性会识别出不同的领先模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图基础模型基准主要关注下游预测性能，难以洞察模型内部表示是否能被忠实分解、临床解释或跨独立分析复现，亟需一个受控、可复现的框架从互补维度评估表示的可解释性。

Method: 引入 ECG-InterpBench，采用稀疏自编码器作为标准化测量仪器，并在模型间匹配字典容量以实现受控比较；评估六个冻结的 ECG 基础模型，覆盖五个编码器深度、五个匹配字典宽度、三个随机种子，形成 450 单元的可解释性图谱（含 75 个精确匹配的六模型对比块）。评估维度包括稀疏重建保真度、单特征可访问性与 49 项临床心电测量的覆盖率、跨种子特征可复现性；同时量化患者采样不确定性、深度与种子依赖的变异、稀疏度参数化的敏感性。

Result: 基准揭示不同 ECG 基础模型呈现显著不同的可解释性画像；在 MIMIC-IV-ECG 上的匹配复现实验证实，重建保真度与临床可访问性会指出不同的领先模型。随基准发布的还有可执行评估代码、标准化清单、单元级指标和可复现性审计。

Conclusion: ECG-InterpBench 通过提供容量受控、可复现的框架，从表示可解释性的多个互补维度对比 ECG 基础模型，有效补充了以性能为中心的现有 ECG 基准。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for electrocardiogram foundation models primarily evaluate downstream predictive performance, providing limited insight into whether their internal representations can be faithfully decomposed, clinically interpreted, or reproduced across independent analyses. We introduce ECG-InterpBench, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the interpretability of ECG foundation-model representations. ECG-InterpBench uses sparse autoencoders as standardized measurement instruments and matches their capacity across models to enable controlled comparisons. We evaluate six frozen ECG foundation models across five standardized encoder depths, five matched dictionary widths, and three random seeds, producing a 450-cell interpretability atlas comprising 75 exactly matched six-model comparison blocks. The benchmark evaluates complementary dimensions of representation interpretability, including sparse reconstruction fidelity, single-feature accessibility and coverage of 49 clinically meaningful ECG measurements, and cross-seed feature reproducibility. The evaluation further quantifies patient-sampling uncertainty, depth- and seed-dependent variation, and sensitivity to the sparsity parameterization. The benchmark reveals that ECG foundation models exhibit distinct interpretability profiles. A matched replication on MIMIC-IV-ECG confirms that reconstruction fidelity and clinical accessibility identify different leading models. The benchmark is accompanied by executable evaluation code, standardized manifests, cell-level metrics, and reproducibility audits. ECG-InterpBench complements performance-centered ECG benchmarks by providing a capacity-controlled and reproducible framework for comparing ECG foundation models across distinct dimensions of representation interpretability.

</details>


### [197] [Context-Informed Ship Trajectory Prediction via Conditional Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27418)
*Yuan Guan,Chandler Squires,Timothy Hu,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conditional Informer，一种编码器-解码器架构，将船舶轨迹预测建模为条件生成任务，引入Conditional Attention机制让船舶状态显式查询环境上下文，并提出Modality Masking训练策略解决真实数据间歇性问题。在AIS和ERA5数据上实验表明，该方法在上下文可用时预测精度提升15.4%，且Modality Masking将回退误差降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的船舶轨迹预测方法主要依赖历史运动学状态，将船舶运动视为孤立系统，忽略了天气等外在因素对航行的深刻调制作用，以及静态船舶特性的约束。现有多模态方法将环境变量视为同级特征，未编码船舶动力学对环境条件的定向物理依赖关系。

Method: 提出Conditional Informer编码器-解码器架构，将轨迹预测建模为条件生成任务。设计Conditional Attention机制，船舶状态通过交叉注意力显式查询环境上下文，编码天气调制但不生成船舶动力学的物理先验。引入Modality Masking训练策略，随机屏蔽模态输入，防止传感器回退时的灾难性退化和捷径学习。

Result: 在AIS和ERA5数据集上的大量实验表明，当上下文可用时，该方法比运动学和拼接基线的预测精度提高15.4%。关键在于Modality Masking防止了捷径学习，与无约束模型相比，回退误差降低近一个数量级。

Conclusion: Conditional Informer通过显式建模环境条件对船舶动力学的条件依赖，结合Modality Masking增强鲁棒性，显著提升了长程船舶轨迹预测的准确性和在真实间歇性数据下的可靠性，为海上安全和自主导航提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term ship trajectory prediction is a fundamental capability for maritime safety and autonomous navigation. While recent Transformer-based architectures have improved forecasting horizons, they predominantly rely on historical kinematic states, treating vessel motion as an isolated system. In reality, maritime navigation is profoundly modulated by extrinsic factors like weather and constrained by static vessel characteristics. Existing multimodal approaches fundamentally model the joint distribution over states and contexts, treating environmental variables as peer features rather than encoding the directional physical dependence of vessel dynamics on environmental conditions.
  In this work, we propose the Conditional Informer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture that formulates trajectory prediction as a conditional generation task. We employ a dedicated Conditional Attention mechanism where the vessel state explicitly queries environmental contexts through cross-attention, encoding the physical prior that weather modulates - but is not generated by - vessel dynamics. Furthermore, to address the intermittency of real-world data, we introduce a Modality Masking training strategy to prevent catastrophic degradation during sensor fallback. Extensive experiments on AIS and ERA5 data demonstrate that our approach outperforms kinematic and concatenation-based baselines by 15.4% in prediction accuracy when context is available. Crucially, Modality Masking prevents shortcut learning, reducing fallback error by nearly an order of magnitude compared to unconstrained models.

</details>


### [198] [Good Rankers, Bad Objectives: Bilinear Contrastive Critics under Expressive Policy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27422)
*Ayushman Singh,Siddharth Aphale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出对比式评论器在动作选择中的局限性：余弦相似度绑定无法消除离支撑集动作的高估问题，范数漂移导致双线性得分失真，导致评论器在导航任务中校准不良甚至反转。Bellman 训练的 TD-Q 表现优异，而对比式评论器仅适合作为兼容性排序器，动作选择仍需价值校准的标量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对比式评论器作为价值目标用于最佳-K 选择、规划和评论器引导生成时的安全性，揭示其在离支撑集动作上的高估机制及实际代价。

Method: 采用受控支撑集分解分析双线性遗憾来源，对比余弦、混合评论器与 Bellman 训练的 TD-Q 在四个 OGBench 导航任务中的表现，通过模拟器回放测量单步选择代价，并进行训练/读出分解追踪排序能力丢失的原因。

Result: 余弦和混合评论器在大多数动作池中仍选择离支撑集动作且遗憾相当；对比式得分在高分十分位弱校准或反转，无法按价值排序固定查询动作；TD-Q 成功且参数匹配的函数类控制也有效；PointMaze 和精确-Q* 玩具任务显示单步选择代价，AntMaze 和 HumanoidMaze 因控制器可自我修正而呈现高功效零结果；训练目标导致排序丢失，原始嵌入经推理时归一化仍保留弱排序。

Conclusion: 候选最大化会利用范数漂移、得分饱和或支撑集内误排序产生的假阳性；对比式评论器在导航和操作任务中仍可作为兼容性排序器，但动作选择需要价值校准的标量评论器。

Abstract: Good action rankings do not make a contrastive critic safe to maximize. These critics increasingly act as value-like objectives for best-of-$K$ selection, planning, and critic-guided generation. Unbounded bilinear scores can let large embedding norms inflate off-support values, but cosine bounding does not remove the failure. A controlled support decomposition attributes most raw bilinear regret to norm drift. Cosine and hybrid critics nevertheless select off-support actions from most pools and incur comparable regret. Contrastive scores are weakly calibrated or inverted in the top score decile across four OGBench navigation tasks, and they fail to order fixed-query actions by value. Bellman-trained TD-Q succeeds, including in a parameter-matched function-class control. Realized costs depend on the task: simulator rollouts reveal single-step selection costs on PointMaze and the exact-$Q^*$ toy but well-powered nulls on AntMaze and HumanoidMaze, where the controller can self-correct. A training/readout decomposition traces the lost ordering to the cosine training objective; raw-trained embeddings retain weak ordering after inference-time normalization. Candidate maximization can therefore exploit false positives caused by norm drift, score saturation, or in-support misranking. Contrastive critics remain useful compatibility rankers on navigation and manipulation tasks, but action selection requires a value-calibrated scalar.

</details>


### [199] [SE(3)-MeanFlow: Few-Step Protein Backbone Generation on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27431)
*Yikun Bai,Binghang Lu,Yikai Liu,Elaheh Akbari,Soheil Kolouri,Linxuan Wang,Ping He,Shuchan Wang,Ruqi Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SE(3)-MeanFlow，一种在蛋白质骨架生成任务上仅需极少采样步数即可达到或超越现有流匹配基线的生成框架。该方法将 MeanFlow 从欧氏空间推广到蛋白质骨架框架的李群几何 SE(3) 上，在李代数 so(3) 和 R^3 中推导出旋转与平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，实现无仿真训练目标；引入 SE(3) alpha-Flow 目标消除旋转分支的 Jacobian-向量积作为热身阶段，随后切换到小 t 稳定化的 MeanFlow 损失完成预训练和基于修正的后训练。实验表明，在少步采样 regime 下 SE(3)-MeanFlow 在相同计算预算下均领先于基线，仅以适度的多样性损失为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散和流匹配模型虽然能在 SE(3)^N 上生成高质量蛋白质骨架，但推理需要数值积分 ODE，涉及数百次网络评估和李群指数映射，成为高通量设计的瓶颈。因此需要一种少步甚至单步的生成框架，直接在蛋白质骨架的李群几何上工作，避免昂贵的数值积分。

Method: 1. 将 MeanFlow 从欧氏空间扩展到 SE(3) 李群几何，原生在李代数 so(3) 和 R^3 中操作。2. 推导旋转和平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，获得无需仿真的训练目标。3. 提出 SE(3) alpha-Flow 目标，移除旋转分支的 Jacobian-向量积，作为预训练热身阶段。4. 切换到小 t 稳定化的 MeanFlow 损失完成剩余预训练，并用于基于修正的后训练。

Result: 在蛋白质骨架生成任务上，SE(3)-MeanFlow 以远少于基线的采样步数达到或超过流匹配基线的性能；在少步采样 regime 下，修正后的模型在每个匹配的计算预算下均领先于基线，仅以适度的多样性下降为代价。

Conclusion: SE(3)-MeanFlow 成功将 MeanFlow 推广到蛋白质骨架的李群几何，通过闭式平均速度目标和分阶段训练策略，实现了极高效的少步生成，显著降低了推理计算成本，为高通量蛋白质设计提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative modeling of protein backbones promises the de novo design of proteins with prescribed structural and functional properties. Existing diffusion and flow-matching models produce high-quality backbones on SE(3)^N, but inference requires numerically integrating an ODE over hundreds of network evaluations, each involving a Lie group exponential map - a bottleneck for high-throughput design campaigns. We introduce SE(3)-MeanFlow, a few-step generative framework that extends MeanFlow from Euclidean space to the Lie group geometry of protein frames. Working natively in the Lie algebra so(3) and in R^3, we derive closed-form average-velocity identities for rotations and translations, giving simulation-free training targets. We further introduce an SE(3) alpha-Flow objective that removes the Jacobian-vector product from the rotation branch and serves as a warm-up stage, after which training switches to a small-t stabilized MeanFlow loss that is used for the remainder of pretraining and for rectification-based post-training. In protein backbone generation, SE(3)-MeanFlow matches or exceeds flow-matching baselines that use several times more sampling steps, and its advantage widens in the few-step regime, where rectification lets it lead at every matched budget - at a modest cost in diversity.

</details>


### [200] [Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN for Channel Modeling in Low-SNR Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27450)
*Chaofan Deng,Linyu Sun,Jaeho Lee,Arijit Raychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为NN-CLEAN的混合框架，将多头残差网络嵌入迭代CLEAN提取循环，替代穷举网格搜索，实现了低信噪比下高精度多径参数估计，且计算复杂度大幅降低，具备实时并行处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大似然估计算法（如CLEAN）虽提供高分辨率参数提取，但因穷举网格搜索导致计算复杂度过高；纯数据驱动深度学习方法缺乏物理先验，难以泛化到变化的多径密度和离网参数。因此需要一种结合物理模型与神经网络的混合方法。

Method: 提出神经网络辅助CLEAN（NN-CLEAN），将多头残差网络直接嵌入迭代CLEAN提取循环。利用神经网络快速并行前向传播替代穷举网格搜索，残差减法委托给精确数学模型，从而在不累积非物理误差的情况下分离物理多径参数。

Result: 大量蒙特卡洛仿真表明，NN-CLEAN在5 dB信噪比下估计准确率超过96%，匹配传统网格搜索CLEAN基线，同时大幅降低计算复杂度，显著优于子空间方法和独立单次神经网络。关键在于，随着批量大小增加，执行运行时间和内存消耗呈近平坦扩展，确立了其作为MIMO系统信道估计鲁棒实时解决方案的地位。

Conclusion: NN-CLEAN通过融合物理模型与深度学习，实现了低信噪比下高精度、低复杂度、可并行化的多径参数估计，为现代无线通信系统（特别是MIMO）的实时信道估计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate multipath parameter estimation is critical for modern wireless communication systems, particularly in challenging low-SNR environments. Traditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation algorithms, such as CLEAN, provide high-resolution parameter extraction but suffer from prohibitive computational complexity due to exhaustive grid search. Conversely, purely data-driven deep learning approaches lack physical grounding and struggle to generalize across variable multipath densities and off-grid parameters. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), a hybrid framework that embeds a multi-head residual network directly into the iterative CLEAN extraction loop. By replacing the exhaustive grid search with rapid, parallelizable forward passes while delegating residual subtraction to exact mathematical models, NN-CLEAN isolates physical multipath parameters without accumulating non- physical errors. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that NN-CLEAN achieves estimation accuracy exceeding 96% at 5 dB SNR, matching the traditional Grid-Search CLEAN (GS- CLEAN) baseline, while providing a massive reduction in computational complexity and substantially outperforming subspace methods and standalone one-shot neural networks. Crucially, NN-CLEAN exhibits a near-flat scaling in execution runtime and memory consumption as batch sizes increase. This highly efficient parallelization establishes NN-CLEAN as a robust, real- time solution for channel estimation in MIMO systems.

</details>


### [201] [FADEx: Feature Attribution and Distortion-based Explanation of Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27463)
*Lucas Greff Meneses,Evandro S. Ortigossa,Claudio Silva,Luis Gustavo Nonato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FADEx，一种基于一阶泰勒展开和奇异值分解的局部逐实例特征归因方法，用于解释任意降维（DR）方法的投影结果。通过加权最小二乘拟合局部线性模型，FADEx 无需外部样本映射，即可提供逐特征归因与失真分析，实验表明其解释更稳健可靠，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性降维技术常作为不透明变换，难以理解单个特征如何影响实例在低维空间中的位置；现有 DR 解释方法存在每个特征多重归因、仅适用于特定 DR 算法等局限，阻碍了广泛应用。

Method: FADEx 采用一阶泰勒展开进行局部线性近似，利用奇异值分解计算局部线性模型，通过加权最小二乘拟合权重，实现与 DR 方法无关的局部特征归因与失真分析，无需额外的 out-of-sample 映射。

Result: 定性与定量评估、对比实验及案例研究均表明，FADEx 能提供稳健、可靠的解释，在多个方面优于现有 DR 解释方法，展现出良好的通用性与有效性。

Conclusion: FADEx 解决了现有 DR 解释方法的关键局限，提供了一种模型无关、逐实例的特征归因框架，为高维数据探索与不透明模型解释提供了可靠的分析工具。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) is a fundamental tool for high-dimensional data exploration, reducing the complexity of latent spaces of machine learning models, and assisting in the explanation of complex opaque models. However, non-linear DR techniques often function as opaque transformations themselves, making it challenging to understand how individual features influence instance positioning in the reduced space. This lack of transparency complicates the analysis and interpretation of structural patterns, hindering the ability to reason about the organization of high-dimensional data based on the projected layout. In order to address this challenge, dimensionality reduction explanation methods have shown promise in improving the understanding of the observed groups and cluster structures. Unfortunately, existing DR explanation approaches tend to suffer from limitations such as multiple attributions per feature and restricted applicability to specific dimensionality reduction methods, which hinder their use. In this work, we propose FADEx, a novel local per-instance feature attribution method that leverages local linear approximation via first-order Taylor expansion and Singular Value Decomposition to provide explanations. FADEx computes the local linear models via weighted least squares, eliminating the need for out-of-sample data mapping, making it agnostic to the DR method, while simultaneously providing local feature attributions and distortion analysis. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations, comparisons with existing methods, and case studies, we demonstrate FADEx's effectiveness and versatility in providing explanations and analytical resources for analyzing the behavior of DR methods. The results indicate FADEx yields robust and reliable explanations, outperforming existing approaches in several aspects.

</details>


### [202] [Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27479)
*Aryan Mishra,Randy Martinez,Lizhen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于群论的图重连方法——Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring (SCGR)，通过从特殊线性群导出的Schreier-Coset图增强输入图，提供理论保证（谱间隙、有效电阻有界），为长距离通信创建低电阻旁路，有效缓解过度压缩问题，在多种任务上将有效电阻降低5-40%且保持竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）的信息流受限于过度压缩，结构瓶颈阻碍长距离信息传播。现有图重连方法虽被广泛使用，但常引入结构和计算瓶颈，难以保持原图关键性质，且大幅增加边数量。

Method: 提出Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring (SCGR)，一种群论重连方法：从特殊线性群导出Schreier-Coset图并增强输入图。该方法提供理论保证，构建的图具有谱间隙和有界有效电阻，为长距离通信创建低电阻旁路。

Result: 实验评估显示SCGR在各类学习任务上将有效电阻降低5-40%，有效缓解连通性瓶颈，同时保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: SCGR通过群论方法解决GNN过度压缩问题，在理论保证和实证性能上均优于现有重连方法，为长距离信息传播提供了高效、结构保持的解决方案。

Abstract: The information flow in the graph neural networks (GNNs) is fundamentally constrained by over-squashing, where structural bottlenecks impede long range information propagation. Graph-rewiring methods, which modify graph topology, have been extensively used to alleviate this. However, existing approaches often introduce prohibitive structural and computational bottlenecks, fail to preserve the critical properties of original graphs, and increase the edge counts massively. We introduce a novel method Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring , a group-theoretic rewiring method that augments the input graph with a Schreier-Coset graph derived from a special linear group. Our method provides theoretical guarantees, a graph that exhibits spectral gap and a bounded effective resistance, creating a low-resistance bypass for long-range communication. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that SCGR reduces effective resistance by 5-40% across various learning tasks, effectively mitigating connectivity bottlenecks while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [203] [Latent States in Neural Networks: Recovering the Temporal Structure of Drifting Data from Model Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27482)
*Kevin Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）从时间流上训练的模型权重轨迹中恢复离散的潜在状态，并在多模态谣言检测和情感分析任务上验证，发现同一状态内的模型泛化能力优于跨状态，且该优势不仅源于标签分布变化，还捕捉了迁移相关的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨时间漂移数据流是否经过离散的机制而非连续变化，并尝试从模型权重轨迹中恢复这些隐含的机制。

Method: 方法是在连续时间窗口上训练分类器，将按时间顺序排列的对齐权重轨迹输入HMM，提取潜在状态以将时间线划分为连贯阶段，并在Fakeddit和Yelp两个数据集上评估跨窗口泛化能力。

Result: 结果显示，同一潜在状态内的窗口间模型泛化优于跨状态窗口，该优势在控制时间邻近性后依然存在，且优于等大小连续分区的基线；状态虽仅从权重估计，但与类别分布变化相关性更强；残差化类别分歧和滞后后，优势仍显著超过排列检验零假设，表明状态捕捉了超越数据分布的迁移相关结构；所有效果在两个任务上复现，但Yelp上减弱，因其标签分布更稳定。

Conclusion: 结论是模型权重轨迹中的隐马尔可夫状态能有效捕捉时间漂移流中的离散机制，这些机制对跨时间窗口的迁移学习有指导意义，且不仅反映标签分布变化，还包含额外的结构信息。

Abstract: A temporally drifting data stream may pass through discrete regimes rather than changing continuously. We ask whether such regimes are recoverable from the weights of models trained on the stream, using a hidden Markov model (HMM) fit to the chronologically ordered trajectory of those weights. We study this question in two domains known to drift over time: multimodal misinformation detection, using the Fakeddit dataset; and sentiment analysis, using the Yelp dataset. We train classifiers on consecutive temporal windows and fit an HMM to the trajectory of their aligned weights, recovering latent states that partition each timeline into coherent phases. On both datasets, classifiers generalize better to data from windows sharing the state of their training window than to windows across state boundaries. This within-state transfer advantage survives a control for temporal proximity and modestly exceeds the advantage recovered by a naive partition into contiguous states of equal size. Although the states are estimated solely from model weights, they correlate more strongly with shifts in the data's class distribution than with the weight-space geometry used to estimate them. After class divergence and lag are residualized out, the within-state advantage exceeds its permutation null on both tasks, indicating that the states recover structure relevant to transfer beyond the data distribution. Every effect replicates on both tasks but is attenuated on Yelp, whose label distribution is more temporally stable.

</details>


### [204] [A Lightweight Foundation Model for Collider Physics with Multi-Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27501)
*Liangyu Wu,Qibin Liu,Alexander Yue,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种轻量级基础模型NEXUS，利用全连接自编码器（约300万参数）在大型强子对撞机碰撞数据上无监督预训练，迁移到下游任务（动能回归、事件分类）及跨域任务（引力波、洪水预报、神经活动），在小标注数据集上显著提升精度，且计算效率高于同规模Transformer，适合实时/边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决科学领域基础模型部署中的计算资源限制问题，通过轻量级架构实现跨域迁移学习，降低对大规模标注数据的依赖，并探索预训练在科学数据中的潜在空间可解释性。

Method: 采用全连接自编码器（约300万参数），在LHC带电粒子轨迹特征数据上进行无监督预训练；随后冻结或微调预训练权重，应用于对撞机下游任务（动能回归、事件分类）及跨域任务（引力波检测、洪水预报、神经活动分析）；通过潜在空间可视化分析预训练效果，并与同规模Transformer对比计算复杂度。

Result: 预训练模型在仅使用少量标注数据的下游任务中显著优于从头训练的同架构模型；潜在空间展示出物理意义的聚类结构；成功迁移至引力波、洪水预报、神经活动等异构科学领域；计算复杂度远低于同规模Transformer，推理功耗更低，支持实时/边缘部署。

Conclusion: NEXUS证明了轻量级自编码器在大规模无标注科学数据上预训练可构建通用基础模型，实现跨域迁移且大幅降低计算开销，为科学实验中的实时推理和边缘计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a lightweight approach to foundation modeling (\textbf{NEXUS}) that leverages pre-trained learning from collider physics data towards out-of-domain tasks in other scientific datasets, using a fully connected autoencoder model with approximately 3 million parameters. The model pre-trains with no supervision over a large-scale collision dataset from the Large Hadron Collider modeled by charged particle track features. Downstream tasks for collider analyses, such as kinematic regression and event classification, are developed on pre-trained model weights and achieve improved accuracy with only small labeled datasets when compared to equivalent architectures trained from scratch. The benefits of pre-training are additionally investigated through latent space interpretation and application to other domains, including gravitational waves, flood forecasting, and neural activity. Furthermore, the relative computational simplicity of NEXUS is demonstrated compared to transformer approaches at comparable scale, opening the door to power-efficient inference and real-time or edge applications of foundation models in scientific experiments.

</details>
