Method: 将空间分解为空三角形，将每个三角形与其高维对应物比较以计算变形，逐三角形变形可聚合为标量值或叠加在投影上可视化区域畸变模式。

Result: 实验表明，GI对具有高视觉影响的小结构畸变敏感，且计算快速、可解释性强，优于流行质量度量。

Conclusion: GI填补了现有度量忽视空白区域畸变的空白，为降维投影的视觉分析提供了更可靠的质量评估工具。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [273] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLAMECHE 将基于元数据的聚类联邦学习重构为分布式期望最大化过程，仅限制服务器执行加法操作，兼容安全联邦学习方案，在隐私保护、通信开销与计算效率的三难困境中取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类联邦学习方法在隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间存在三难困境；基于元数据的聚类虽高效，但不兼容标准联邦学习隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出 FLAMECHE，将基于元数据的 CFL 重新表述为分布式期望最大化（EM）过程，将服务器更新限制为加法操作，保持效率并兼容加密方案。

Result: 在多个数据集和异构场景下的大量实验表明，FLAMECHE 提升了客户端模型的有效性，实现了加密兼容的基于元数据的聚类，改善了在 CFL 三难困境中的定位。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE 通过分布式 EM 重构实现了隐私保护与效率的平衡，为聚类联邦学习提供了兼容安全聚合的实用方案。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [274] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 LedgerMind 框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为带有 Structured Evidence Ledger（结构化证据账本）的 provenance- 减少那些看起来毫无帮那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些那些个


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态智能体评估主要依赖最终答案准确率，无法区分正确答案是基于有据可查的证据、语言先验还是意外错误抵消获得的，无法评估推理轨迹的忠实性。

Method: 提出 LedgerMind 框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为 provenance-constrained state machine：工具输出归一化为 Structured Evidence Ledger 作为轨迹状态，下游推理和决策只能引用活跃的账本条目，在实体和数值层面进行 grounding 检查，修复实现为带类型的状态转移，禁止在没有工具产生 provenance 的情况下引入内容。配套 Three-Layer Grounding Protocol、Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher 和 Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair 引擎并提供 formal provenance non-amplification guarantee。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和多个 backbone MLLM 上实验表明，LedgerMind 同时提升了最终答案准确率和轨迹级忠实度。

Conclusion: LedgerMind 通过 Structured Evidence Ledger 和 provenance-constrained state machine 机制，实现了可验证、可修复的多模态智能体推理轨迹，同时提升了答案准确率和轨迹级忠实度，解决了最终答案准确率无法揭示的四类典型失效模式。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [275] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家的先验-对偶自然演员-评论家算法，解决了平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程中神经评论家的偏差-成本权衡难题，实现了$̃{O}(T^{-1/2})$阶的最优性差距和约束违背，建立了首个无需混合时间的通用策略参数化下的阶最优收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中，约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）要求智能体在满足长期约束的同时最大化长期奖励。虽然线性评论家的先验-对偶演员-评论家方法已被充分理解，但将阶最优收敛保证扩展到平均奖励CMDP中的神经评论家一直是一个开放问题。核心挑战在于神经切线核（NTK）分析下的根本偏差-成本权衡：降低评论家偏差会显著增加优化成本，阻碍了先验-对偶框架中的阶最优收敛。

Method: 引入分层多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家，同时在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个层面进行去偏估计。该估计器仅以对数级期望采样成本达到长优化运行的偏差水平。基于此估计器，开发了先验-对偶自然演员-评论家算法。

Result: 该算法实现了$̃{O}(T^{-1/2})$阶的最优性差距和约束违背。这是针对无限时域平均奖励CMDP、通用策略参数化和神经评论家的首个阶最优收敛保证，且无需知晓底层混合时间。即使在无约束设置下，该结果也是新颖的。

Conclusion: 本文通过分层MLMC神经评论家解决了偏差-成本权衡瓶颈，为平均奖励CMDP中的神经评论家建立了首个阶最优收敛理论，消除了对混合时间的依赖，具有重要理论意义和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [276] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT），通过在空闲期预计算有界条件策略树，消除计算机使用代理在瞬态GUI事件中的自回归解码延迟，显著提高决策窗口内的成功率（从0.50到0.79），且不产生错误动作。实验表明AAPT在候选动作可预先枚举时表现最佳，而反应式执行在动作不可预先枚举时更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理在处理瞬态GUI事件时常因自回归解码延迟导致在相关窗口关闭后才产生正确动作，主因是昂贵的自回归解码位于决策时关键路径上。

Method: 提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT）：在空闲屏幕期间，冻结的多模态模型构建有界条件策略树，包含可观测守卫、预授权动作和分支专用截止时间；树规模覆盖模型自身解码延迟。事件发生时，轻量观察者将变化门控帧匹配到准备好的分支，立即执行对应动作而无需生成新文本。

Result: 配对试验中AAPT将争用决策窗口内成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），零错误动作；开环和预测-重规划基线成功率为零。准备时间扫描显示增益出现在基于延迟的树规模规则预测处；消融实验揭示三大关键需求：快速观察者解码、有效树规划、准确分支路由。预注册神谕探针否定初始假设，指向分支路由为因果瓶颈。在独立通用多模态模型上126次配对试验复现效果（p=4.9×10⁻¹³）。外部基准上AAPT整体性能与反应式基线持平，两者优势互补。

Conclusion: AAPT在候选动作可预先枚举时表现最佳；当动作不可预先枚举时，反应式执行仍更强。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [277] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模动作空间下的策略学习，针对在线策略和离线策略两种范式，提出结构化贝叶斯方法和结构化直接方法及悲观估计器，实现高效探索与偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动作空间导致探索低效、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重高方差、外推偏差及优化困难，现有方法难以有效共享动作间信息并控制偏差-方差权衡。

Method: 第一部分提出 meTS（混合效应 Thompson 采样）和 dTS（基于扩散先验的 Thompson 采样），利用结构化先验共享动作间信息；第二部分提出 sDM（基于潜变量的结构化直接方法），设计凹且可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标；最后开发基于指数平滑和 PAC-贝叶斯界的可微悲观方法，控制正则化重要性采样估计器的偏差-方差。

Result: meTS 和 dTS 实现依赖于有效动作数而非总动作数的遗憾界；sDM 揭示优化误差在大动作空间可主导估计误差，并给出高效优化目标；悲观方法提供可微的偏差-方差控制，提升离线策略学习性能。

Conclusion: 通过结构化建模与悲观估计，本文在大规模动作空间的上下文赌博机中统一解决了在线探索与离线评估的核心挑战，为实际大规模决策系统提供了理论保证与实用算法。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [278] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu,Xiaoyu Hu,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 QQWorld，用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代 Epps-Pulley 目标，改善潜在空间高斯对齐，提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM 使用 EP 目标正则化潜在分布，但 EP 对孤立尾部样本的修正梯度快速消失，导致重尾偏差控制不足。

Method: 提出 QQWorld，采用分位数-分位数匹配目标，将投影潜在样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数直接对齐，保持尾部有效修正梯度；并开发跨批次 QQ，利用前序批次的分离样本扩大排序池，分析其偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld 提升 LeWM 平均规划成功率，持续获得更好的高斯对齐和更薄的潜在尾部。

Conclusion: QQWorld 通过 QQ 匹配目标有效解决 EP 目标在尾部梯度消失的问题，改善潜在分布高斯性，从而提升规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>


### [279] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出QAdapt，一种用于表面码量子纠错的噪声自适应神经预解码框架，能捕捉综合征数据的局部时空相关性，顺序适应不断变化的噪声条件并缓解灾难性遗忘，将残余综合征传递给传统全局解码器。在合成分布外噪声配置和Google Willow基准数据上，QAdapt持续降低逻辑错误率并减少后端解码延迟，展示了在不断变化的硬件噪声下提升量子纠错鲁棒性和解码效率的实用且兼容解码器的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算（FTQC）依赖量子纠错抑制物理错误，但实际性能受限于物理噪声和经典解码器处理快速生成的综合征数据的延迟。硬件噪声具有强、异构、非平稳特性，且仿真到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能。

Method: QAdapt捕捉综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应不断演变的噪声条件同时缓解灾难性遗忘，并将残余综合征转发给传统的全局解码器。

Result: 在110种合成的分布外噪声配置（旋转表面码存储电路）上，QAdapt相对于神经预解码基线持续降低逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上，无需目标域微调，实现逻辑错误率最高降低5.79%，后端残余综合征解码延迟降低9.32%。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且兼容解码器的方法，能在不断变化的硬件噪声下提升量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [280] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入(KSSE)，一种基于物理启发的能量模型，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代密集CNN分类器，在ImageNet-1000上以21.24M参数达88.93% Top-1准确率，显著优于同规模模型并匹配大型ViT，模型体积减少10-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决深度学习分类器参数量大、计算密集的问题，利用物理启发的稀疏图谱方法实现高效、高精度的分类，同时提供理论保证。

Method: 将预训练特征映射至准循环低密度校验图，构建正则化拉普拉斯算子作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量，利用FFT在循环块上求解谱问题，采用星域手术优化图拓扑，通过多尺度分形分析验证地形转变，结合Rayleigh细化实现高效推理。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000上冻结EfficientNet-B4特征(D=1792)的归纳式协议中，KSSE以21.24M参数达88.93% Top-1准确率，优于Swin-L(197M, 86.4-87.3%)并匹配ViT-H/14(632M, 88.0-89.5%)，模型体积分别减少10倍和30倍。并证明了六个理论结果：广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、捕获集本征值对应、加性通道可分性及交换关联界、手术定理挫折界、准平稳扰动界、不动点收敛定理。

Conclusion: KSSE展示了基于物理的稀疏图谱嵌入在大规模视觉分类中的高效性与理论可靠性，为轻量化高性能模型提供新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [281] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种短期图记忆模块，作为即插即用组件集成到分子优化生成器中，利用在线图神经网络代理模型预筛选候选分子，在固定Oracle调用预算下优先评估高效用分子，从而提升优化性能。实验表明该模块在多种生成器和预算设定下均能提升平均Top-10分数，且从不降低基线性能。进一步分析揭示了代理引导选择与生成器探索-利用行为的交互规律，指出了受益生成器的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化常在有限Oracle调用预算下进行，如何决定评估哪些分子与生成哪些分子同等重要。现有方法主要关注生成策略，而忽视了在固定预算下更有选择性地使用Oracle的机会。需要一种即插即用的方法，在不改变生成器架构和原生更新规则的前提下，利用历史评估信息优先查询高价值候选分子。

Method: 提出短期图记忆模块，维护一个在线图神经网络代理模型，对每轮候选池进行预筛选，仅将预测效用最高的分子提交给Oracle评估。该模块保留生成器原有架构和更新规则，作为即插即用组件集成。在标准分子优化基准上，应用于基于片段的生成器及其他三种生成器进行实验验证。

Result: 在紧预算（1000次Oracle调用）下，该模块使所有四种测试生成器的平均Top-10分数均有提升，且从不落后于基线。在更大预算下，收益与生成器主干的两个特性一致：搜索广度和原生搜索利用Oracle反馈的有效性。提供了一种简单的固定预算更选择性使用方法，并给出了哪些生成器受益的实证证据。

Conclusion: 短期图记忆通过在线代理模型预筛选候选分子，在不增加Oracle成本前提下显著提升分子优化效率。该方法具有通用性，适用于多种生成器架构，且能自适应地与生成器的探索-利用特性配合。研究为有限预算下的分子优化提供了实用的选择性评估策略，并揭示了生成器特性与代理引导收益的关系。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [282] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于思维链推理的分类器，用于安全运营中心的告警分类，结合自动提示优化、自我训练和可验证奖励的强化学习，并训练校准器估计裁决正确性，显著提高了高置信度下的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临告警疲劳问题，现有工作直接让大语言模型输出分类标签，但缺乏推理能力，无法判断检测是否为真实威胁。

Method: 在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上训练思维链推理分类器，结合自动提示优化、自我训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习；并单独训练校准器读取完整推理轨迹估计裁决正确概率。

Result: 测试准确率达到82.6%，在高置信度操作点下，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%；校准器至关重要，未训练的置信度判断器会导致高置信度召回归零；微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。

Conclusion: 针对性训练优于单纯扩大模型规模，思维链推理结合校准机制能有效缓解告警疲劳，实现高置信度下的自动化分类。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [283] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种因果表现性框架用于算法补救，解决现有方法仅关注翻转预测而忽视真实资质改进的问题，通过结构因果模型建模补救行动的传播，实现稳定均衡并减少博弈激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法补救方法仅关注翻转分类器预测，未考虑推荐变更是否真正改善个体真实资质或仅使其规避分类器，导致部署的补救策略诱发行为响应，降低预测准确性且模型重训练后失效。

Method: 构建因果表现性框架，利用结构因果模型捕捉特征间交互及其对真实标签的影响，刻画补救行动如何传播；分析非凸优化问题，给出演算稳定解存在条件及通过简单迭代动态高效计算的方法。

Result: 实验表明，忽略因果结构的补救策略会诱发大规模错位行为响应，而因果补救能达到稳定均衡并减少博弈激励；在半合成和真实信贷数据集上，该方法持续优于标准经验风险最小化，并减少因策略性代理行为导致的分布偏移所需的重复模型重训练。

Conclusion: 因果补救框架能有效解决算法补救中的博弈问题，实现预测性能稳定且减少模型重训练需求，为高风险分类场景提供更可靠的补救方案。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [284] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了同图节点分类（NC）与链接预测（LP）的跨任务迁移，提出了无泄露评估协议，发现迁移具有强方向性：NC→LP在同质图上稳定有益，LP→NC脆弱且可能降精度，仅在结构主导且LP易而NC未饱和时可靠正向，LP充当结构预训练。引入CoTask Score（CTS）量化联合效用，并证实同质度等数据集统计量可指导机制选择、避免负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图常需在同一结构上同时支撑NC与LP等多任务，存在跨任务复用监督的机会，但现有评估常使用不兼容的数据划分、观测图假设和负采样规则，导致关于同图跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。

Method: 形式化同图NC-LP迁移问题，设计无泄露协议：固定节点与边划分，使用排除待评估边的共享消息传递图，LP采用固定负样本。在GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS三种骨干网络上系统实验。

Result: 迁移呈强方向性且可预测：NC→LP在同质图上持续提升性能；LP→NC脆弱，朴素复用表征甚至降精度；仅当LP简单且NC未饱和的结构主导区间，LP→NC才稳定正向，表明LP充当结构预训练。提出CTS汇总联合NC+LP效用，实验表明同质度等简单统计量可指导机制选择、规避负迁移。

Conclusion: 建立了严谨的同图跨任务迁移评估框架，揭示了迁移的方向性本质，提供了联合效用度量CTS，并证明数据集固有统计特性（特别是同质度）能有效预测迁移成败，为多任务图学习的实践提供了可操作的指导原则。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [285] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了原子策略优化（APO），一种完全无监督的对齐框架，通过双奖励机制（主导潜在结构模式奖励 + 热力学稳定性奖励）消除对真实坐标标签的依赖，在晶体和抗体结构预测上超越全监督基线，实现新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）依赖有监督偏好学习对齐真实坐标，但获取新型晶相或从头蛋白的实验标签成本极高，造成数据稀缺场景下结构建模的瓶颈。

Method: APO将群体相对策略优化引入3D原子环境，设计双奖励机制：(i) 通过样本相似性的特征分解强化策略的主导潜在结构模式；(ii) 强制热力学稳定性。模型通过在采样群体中识别物理合理的构型实现“自我修正”。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测基准测试中，APO持续优于全监督基线，达到匹配率和结构保真度的新SOTA；同时有效拉直概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 内在物理一致性比嘈杂的监督坐标匹配能作为更优越的对齐指引，无监督物理驱动对齐在结构预测中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [286] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出β-OPSD，将原始OPSD推广为带可控正则化参数β的策略优化族，通过几何插值混合参考策略与教师策略的logits实现高效蒸馏，并引入return-to-go信用分配对齐序列级目标，在数学推理基准上显著提升稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始OPSD（β=1）在实践中脆弱且需大量工程调优，作者发现其结构性难点在于隐式固定了KL惩罚权重β，缺乏对参考策略与特权教师指导之间权衡的可控性。

Method: 1) 推导β-OPSD最优策略为参考策略与教师策略的几何插值；2) 将闭式解转化为蒸馏目标，通过按β加权混合token级logits高效实现；3) 引入return-to-go信用分配，使token级更新与序列级回报对齐，保持OPSD的简洁性。

Result: 在数学推理基准实验中，β-OPSD持续优于原始OPSD，显著改善优化稳定性并提升下游推理表现。

Conclusion: β-OPSD提供了一条从自蒸馏到策略优化再回蒸馏的 principled 路径，在保持OPSD高效性的同时实现了更稳健、高性能的推理模型训练。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [287] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出KAISEN五阶段审计流程，在合成基准上评估至失效点，揭示四个关键发现：显著性检测与最小可检测效应相关；组别阈值优化稳健降低EOD而Platt校准不可靠；机制诊断在代理变量规范错误时失效且无信号；CUSUM监测阈值难以跨队列迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在整体性能强但不同患者亚组错误率差异大，现有审计流程组件缺乏压力测试，不清楚哪些部分可信及适用条件。

Method: 提出KAISEN五阶段审计流程（亚组分层、差异度量、机制诊断、事后缓解、漂移监测），在包含16疾病任务、15社会决定因素轴、三个预设交集的合成基准上评估至失效点。

Result: 四个发现：(i) 显著性计数与原始EOD秩相关rho=0.56，标准化后升至0.78；(ii) 组别阈值优化48/48降低EOD（均值-0.285），组别Platt校准仅19/48改善，均值接近零；(iii) 机制诊断144/144受控案例正确，但代理变量规范错误时48模型驱动案例全失败且无失效信号；(iv) CUSUM误报和漏报主要源于队列实现而非疾病，阈值难以跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: KAISEN审计流程在合成基准上系统评估揭示了审计组件的可靠性边界：显著性检测依赖最小可检测效应，阈值优化优于校准，机制诊断对代理变量规范错误敏感且无失效预警，漂移监测阈值不具备跨队列泛化性。所有结果基于已知真值的合成数据，不确立临床效度。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


### [288] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种递归Transformer范式，用于小数据工程代理建模，证明了权重共享递归Transformer在参数效率、计算成本和预测精度之间提供了有效的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在工程设计典型的小规模、低维数据集上往往过度参数化，导致过拟合并带来不必要的内存和计算开销，因此需要转向关注额外计算而非额外可学习参数的架构。

Method: 硬件感知评估三种递归Transformer范式：a) Tiny Recursive Model，b) 提出的Depth Recursive Transformer，c) 简单递归Transformer。系统对比其预测性能（Recall、Mean Reciprocal Rank）、参数量、计算复杂度（FLOPs），并在热力学可靠性分析和拉普拉斯PDE求解两个低维工程任务上验证。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在参数效率、计算成本和预测精度之间实现了有效且可泛化的权衡，为资源受限场景下的架构选择提供了实用设计指南。

Conclusion: 递归Transformer通过权重共享机制，在小数据工程代理建模任务中表现出优于传统过参数化Transformer的综合性能，是资源受限环境下的有效选择。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27577)
*Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Zhenwei Tang,Peifan Wu,Nada Al-Thawr,Luoshu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对异构推荐流（如 Google Discover）中的排序挑战，提出 HA-MoE 异构自适应多门控混合专家架构，显式引入异构上下文到门控网络和专家表示中，实现有效专业化且不显著增加开销；配套 LENS 可观测框架提供专家专业化的可解释诊断与持续监控；采用 Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC) 异构感知评估指标。离线大规模工业数据集实验与在线 A/B 测试均验证了该方法在提升整体排序性能、跨段排序正确性、Feed 活跃度及探索指标上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构推荐流（整合网页文章、长/短视频、UGC 等多源内容）面临特征密度差异大、用户交互模式多样、统一排序模型易受负迁移或多数类偏见影响等挑战，现有同构环境下的方法难以直接迁移，亟需一种能在工业规模下有效管理异构性的端到端解决方案。

Method: 1. HA-MoE：异构自适应多门控混合专家架构，将显式异构上下文（如内容类型、来源等）注入门控网络与专家表示，实现专家专业化分工，且参数开销可控。2. LENS：轻量级可观测框架，提供专家专业化的可解释诊断，并跟踪持续再训练过程中的功能异构演变。3. DL-AUC：双层 AUC 评估指标，结合全局排序性能与跨段排序正确性，量化异构场景下的模型表现。

Result: 离线实验在大规模工业数据集上，HA-MoE 在 DL-AUC 及各细分指标上持续超越基线模型。在线 A/B 测试显示 Feed 活跃度与探索指标均显著提升，验证了方法在真实生产环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: HA-MoE 结合 LENS 与 DL-AUC，构建了一个完整的工业级异构推荐排序解决方案，有效解决了异构内容统一排序中的负迁移与多数类偏见问题，实现了专家专业化与模型可观测性的平衡，为大规模异构推荐系统提供了可复用的实践范式。

Abstract: Heterogeneous recommendation feeds present complex challenges that extend beyond those found in highly homogeneous environments (e.g., music-only or video-only closed-ecosystem platforms). In Google Discover, a unified feed integrates diverse content sourced from the decentralized open web, including web articles, long-form and short-form videos, user-generated content (UGC), and beyond. Different content types exhibit distinct feature densities and user interaction patterns. Building a unified ranking model that sustains high performance across such heterogeneity, while avoiding negative transfer or majority bias, remains a significant industrial challenge.
  This paper presents an end-to-end case study on the industrial-scale multi-task ranking of heterogeneous feeds, grounded in real-world deployment. We introduce HA-MoE, a heterogeneity-adaptive multi-gated mixture-of-experts architecture that incorporates explicit heterogeneity context into both gating networks and expert representations. This approach enables effective specialization without significantly increasing operational overhead. To support reliable deployment, we introduce LENS, a lightweight observability framework that provides interpretable diagnostics of expert specialization and tracks this functional heterogeneity across continuous retraining. We evaluate our method using Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC), a heterogeneity-aware evaluation metric that combines global ranking performance with cross-segment ranking correctness. Offline evaluations on a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, online A/B testing confirms gains in feed activity and exploration metrics. Together, offline and online results validate the effectiveness of our approach for managing heterogeneity in industrial-scale recommender systems.

</details>


### [290] [Restoring Collaborative Signals in Semantic-ID Generative Recommendation via Personalized Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27682)
*Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Yaqiang Zang,Jikun Kang,Pinghua Gong,Xue Liu,Bowei He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于个性化自然语言的框架，通过在推理时添加层级协作线索，解决LLM生成式推荐模型中语义ID（SID）无法同时承载内容与协作信号的问题，显著提升推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成式推荐模型将推荐建模为自回归生成语义ID（SID），但SID空间紧凑，难以同时编码内容信息和协作信号，导致两者竞争且协作信号丢失；文本与SID的嵌入空间错位，显式推理难以转化为正确SID，多轮训练效果有限，缺乏在推理时注入协作信号的可靠通道。

Method: 提出一个由个性化自然语言引导的框架，在生成过程中动态添加层级协作线索，不修改骨干网络也不重新训练SID。利用自然语言建立协作模式与受众之间的可分析关联，补全SID缺失的协作信号，而非直接将语言映射到SID。

Result: 在推荐准确率上取得持续提升，将生成过程锚定在推理时的协作结构上，而非依赖显式推理或重新训练。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自然语言引导的层级协作线索，有效解决了SID无法同时承载内容与协作信号的根本缺陷，为LLM生成式推荐提供了一种无需重训练、即插即用的推理时增强方案，显著提升了推荐的个性化与准确性。

Abstract: Making LLM-based generative recommendation models stronger and more personalized through natural language and explicit reasoning is a widely anticipated yet still unsolved goal. Such models cast recommendation as autoregressively generating an item's semantic-ID (SID), a short tuple of discrete codes, so that recommending well reduces to emitting the right SID. In this setting the model verbalizes its knowledge poorly, and text and SID tokens live in misaligned embedding spaces. Deep reasoning therefore rarely turns into a correct SID, and enabling explicit "thinking" often gives no gain or even hurts. The deeper cause is that a compact SID cannot hold content and collaborative signal at once: the two compete, and collaboration loses. Because a mis-predicted SID is a wrong recommendation, this caps accuracy directly. Costly multi-round training barely helps, and few methods try to supply the missing signal at inference time. What is missing is a reliable channel that carries collaborative signal into SID generation. We therefore propose a framework, guided by personalized natural language, that adds hierarchical collaborative cues as the model generates, without altering the backbone or retraining the SIDs. Rather than mapping language onto SIDs directly, it uses language to attach analyzable links between collaborative patterns and their audiences, restoring the collaborative signal that SIDs miss. The result is consistent gains in recommendation accuracy, grounding generation in collaborative structure at inference time rather than relying on explicit reasoning or retraining.

</details>


### [291] [Hierarchical Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27760)
*Peiyu Hu,Siying Gu,Weihai Lu,Zhuodong Liu,Yuntian Tang,Jiahao Liang,Yiying Xie,Jiang Rong,Zhaokai Luo,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HiLaR 是一个面向 LLM 推荐的分层潜在推理框架，采用层感知强化优化，将时间引导的分层用户偏好表征与多层潜在推理状态对齐，并通过边际目标似然增益构建过程奖励优化推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推荐方法存在两大问题：显式自然语言推理带来巨大推理开销；潜在推理方法虽生成或验证中间状态，但未充分刻画各层偏好角色及其贡献，导致推理过程优化不足。

Method: HiLaR 构建时间引导的分层用户偏好表征，将其与 LLM 多层潜在推理状态对齐，组织从宽泛偏好到细粒度当前意图的推理过程；引入层感知过程奖励，基于各状态的边际目标似然增益计算，结合最终推荐反馈联合优化推理轨迹。

Result: 在四个 Amazon 基准数据集上，HiLaR 整体优于强序列、生成式及 LLM 推荐基线；消融与敏感性实验验证了分层表征学习、潜在对齐及过程级优化的贡献。

Conclusion: HiLaR 通过分层潜在推理与层感知强化优化，有效解决了现有 LLM 推荐中推理开销大、层级贡献不明的问题，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation by leveraging their semantic understanding and contextual modeling capabilities. Recent studies further introduce reasoning mechanisms to improve user preference modeling. However, explicit natural-language reasoning incurs substantial inference overhead, whereas existing latent reasoning methods mainly focus on generating or verifying intermediate states, leaving their layer-wise preference roles and contributions insufficiently characterized. We propose HiLaR, a Hierarchical Latent Reasoning framework with layer-aware reinforcement optimization for LLM-based recommendation. HiLaR constructs temporal-guided hierarchical user preference representations, aligns them with multiple LLM latent reasoning states, and organizes the reasoning process from broad preferences to fine-grained current intents. To further optimize the reasoning trajectory, HiLaR combines final recommendation feedback with layer-aware process rewards derived from the marginal target-likelihood gain of each state. Experiments on four Amazon benchmark datasets show that HiLaR generally outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-based recommendation baselines. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further verify the contribution of hierarchical representation learning, latent alignment, and process-level optimization. Our code is available in https://github.com/hupeiyu21/HiLaR.

</details>


### [292] [From Understanding to Action: Feedback-Grounded Policy Discovery for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27789)
*Zhi Chen,Minmao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Haoqiang Liang,Huihuang Lin,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Yulong Wang,Shijie Yi,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对语义ID生成式推荐模型与大语言模型（LLM）结合时的“理解-行动鸿沟”问题，提出了一种反馈驱动的智能体框架：先诱导任务导向的意图知识，再基于结果反馈发现推荐策略知识，最后通过双空间关系蒸馏将意图与策略知识压缩为轻量级语义ID生成器的两个潜在token，实现无LLM的在线推理。公开基准实验与大规模在线A/B测试均显著提升效果，收入与广告视频观看时长分别提升4.506%和4.621%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐模型虽能高效生成下一项目，但其监督信号主要捕捉行为共现与局部转移；LLM虽能推理异构交互历史理解用户当前需求，却缺乏推荐专用的结果反馈训练，语言上合理的推理不一定能转化为有效推荐决策，即存在“理解-行动鸿沟”。需区分意图知识（捕捉用户当前需求）与策略知识（规定推荐方向与拒绝边界），并用结果反馈而非语言合理性来桥接两者。

Method: 提出反馈驱动智能体框架：1）诱导任务导向的意图知识；2）基于意图基线的增量效用发现推荐策略知识，候选策略用结果导向反馈评估优化；3）通过双空间关系蒸馏将意图与策略知识迁移至轻量级语义ID生成器的两个潜在token，实现无LLM在线推理。

Result: 公开基准实验持续超越基线；大规模在线A/B测试中，收入提升4.506%，广告视频观看时长（ADVV）提升4.621%。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分意图与策略知识、引入结果反馈驱动的策略发现、以及双空间蒸馏压缩至轻量生成器，有效弥合了LLM理解能力与推荐决策执行之间的鸿沟，实现了显著的业务指标提升且保持了在线推理效率。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommenders enable efficient next-item generation, but their item-level supervision mainly captures behavioral co-occurrence and local transitions. Large language models (LLMs) can complement these models by reasoning over heterogeneous interaction histories to understand the user's current demand. However, LLMs are not inherently trained with recommendation-specific outcome feedback, and linguistically plausible reasoning therefore does not necessarily lead to effective recommendation decisions. We term this mismatch the Understanding-Action Gap. Accordingly, we distinguish intent knowledge, which captures the user's current demand, from policy knowledge, which specifies the recommendation direction and rejection boundary under that demand. To bridge this gap, we propose a feedback-driven agent framework that first induces task-oriented intent and then discovers recommendation policies according to their incremental utility over an intent-only baseline. Candidate policies are evaluated and refined using outcome-derived feedback rather than linguistic plausibility. We further transfer the resulting intent and policy knowledge into two latent tokens of a lightweight Semantic-ID generator through dual-space relational distillation, enabling LLM-free online inference. Experiments on public benchmarks show consistent improvements over baselines, while large-scale online A/B tests achieve gains of 4.506% in Revenue and 4.621% in ADVV.

</details>


### [293] [Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27944)
*Long Zhang,Hao Jiang,Sheng Yu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 LGRID 方法，通过 LLM 驱动的生成式解耦范式解决语义 ID（SID）生成中的语义纠缠与黑盒表示问题，实现可解释、低碰撞的本地生活服务推荐语义 ID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SID 生成框架采用单表示后量化范式，存在两大瓶颈：语义纠缠混合地理、品牌、类别等异构属性导致量化信息损失、低质量 SID 和严重碰撞；黑盒表示学习无法提供显式属性语义，且 SID 位置缺乏明确地理或语义含义，削弱检索可靠性及 SID 生成的可诊断性和可控性。

Method: LGRID 采用 Encode → Disentangle → Align → Quantize 流水线：联合 LLM 编码保留跨属性地理-语义依赖；结构化解耦模块将隐藏状态路由至地理与语义因素的属性对齐槽位；协同对齐学习使槽位既可生成式解码又具判别式检索能力；双流残差量化分别将两流离散化为紧凑 SID 并保持显式属性对应。

Result: 在 Kuaishou 和 Foursquare 数据集上，LGRID 持续超越强 SID 基线，最高带来 5.44% 相对 AUC 提升；粗粒度地理字段属性解码准确率超 99%，全 SID 碰撞率降至 39.9%，远低于 LGSID 的 97.0%。

Conclusion: LGRID 通过生成式解耦实现了可解释的语义 ID，显著提升推荐性能并大幅降低碰撞率，为本地生活服务推荐提供了可诊断、可控的 SID 生成范式。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced ID-based recommendation through Semantic ID (SID) modeling, existing SID generation frameworks largely follow a single-representation-then-quantization paradigm. This design faces two bottlenecks: semantic entanglement mixes heterogeneous attributes, such as geography, brand, and category, causing information loss during quantization, low-quality SIDs, and severe collisions; moreover, black-box representation learning provides neither explicit attribute semantics nor clear geographic or semantic meanings for SID positions. These limitations weaken both retrieval reliability and the ability to diagnose or control SID generation. We propose Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation (LGRID). LGRID introduces a generative disentanglement paradigm through an Encode -> Disentangle -> Align -> Quantize pipeline. It first uses joint LLM encoding to preserve cross-attribute geographic-semantic dependencies, rather than encoding fields independently. A Structured Disentangled Block then routes hidden states into attribute-aligned slots for geographic and semantic factors. Synergistic Alignment Learning makes these slots both generatively decodable and discriminative for retrieval, while Dual-Stream Residual Quantization separately discretizes the two streams into compact SIDs with explicit attribute correspondence. This design yields interpretable SIDs with positions grounded in item attributes and local-service semantics. Experiments on Kuaishou and Foursquare show that LGRID consistently outperforms strong SID baselines, achieving up to a 5.44 percent relative AUC gain. It also achieves over 99 percent attribute-decoding accuracy for coarse geographic fields and reduces the full-SID collision rate to 39.9 percent, compared with 97.0 percent for LGSID.

</details>


### [294] [VIG-RL: Learning to Search and Insert for Verified Image Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28055)
*Qinhan Yu,Jun Guang,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIG-RL，一种自主智能体框架，用于知识密集型场景下的验证图像接地（VIG）。它将搜索-选择-插入工作流建模为动态决策过程，在 ReAct 风格循环中通过强化学习优化，采用复合奖励系统评估工具执行与多模态对齐，实现新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强框架主要依赖解耦的静态流水线，无法动态推理何时需要外部知识以及视觉素材应在何处上下文插入，导致在知识密集型场景中难以提供可靠的交织文本-图像响应。

Method: 提出 VIG-RL，将搜索-选择-插入流程建模为主动决策过程，在动态 ReAct 风格循环中运行，通过强化学习优化，引入复合奖励系统全面评估智能体逐步工具执行与最终多模态对齐质量。

Result: 大量实验评估表明，VIG-RL 建立了新的最先进水平，显著优于现有静态基线方法。

Conclusion: VIG-RL 通过自主智能体框架和强化学习，有效解决了静态流水线无法动态推理视觉接地时机与位置的问题，为知识密集型场景下的可靠多模态生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: In knowledge-intensive scenarios, providing reliable interleaved text-image responses requires Verified Image Grounding (VIG): the precise integration of retrieved authentic visual evidence. Existing retrieval-augmented frameworks predominantly rely on decoupled, static pipelines, inherently failing to dynamically reason about when external knowledge is required and where visual assets should be contextually inserted. To bridge this gap, we propose VIG-RL, an autonomous agentic framework that formulates the search-selection-insertion workflow as an active decision-making process. Operating within a dynamic ReAct-style loop, VIG-RL is optimized via reinforcement learning, guided by a composite reward system that holistically evaluates the agent's step-by-step tool execution and final multimodal alignment. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VIG-RL establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming existing static baselines.

</details>


### [295] [CCFormer: Efficient Cross-Field Interaction and Hierarchical Sequence Compression for Industrial Recommendation at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28070)
*Yunlong Wang,Huizhe Zhang,Haonan Hu,Yudong Li,Bing Wen,Jianchao Tu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCFormer，一种高效的Transformer骨干网络，统一跨领域特征交互与压缩长序列建模，用于工业推荐系统。通过特征领域分离的交叉注意力和长序列子空间Token混合，利用分层序列压缩策略实现高效长序列建模。在公开基准和腾讯大规模工业数据集上超越SOTA，线上A/B测试显示视频推荐CTR提升3.57%，广告收入提升1.71%，训练加速2.21倍，已全面部署于腾讯生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中，基于自注意力的序列推荐模型虽受益于可预测的缩放定律，但严格的延迟和资源约束使得计算开销与细粒度特征交互难以平衡。现有方法难以同时高效处理跨领域特征交互和长序列建模，亟需一种统一且高效的架构。

Method: 提出CCFormer架构：1) 特征领域分离的交叉注意力，实现跨异构特征领域的细粒度交互；2) 长序列子空间Token混合，捕捉长期偏好信号；3) 分层序列压缩策略，通过逐步扩大感受野实现高效长序列建模并减少信息损失。

Result: 在两个公开基准和腾讯大规模工业数据集上，CCFormer持续超越SOTA基线。线上A/B测试：视频推荐场景CTR提升3.57%，广告排序场景广告收入提升1.71%，训练速度比强基线HSTU加速2.21倍。已全面部署于腾讯主流量推荐系统。

Conclusion: CCFormer通过统一跨领域特征交互与压缩长序列建模，有效解决了工业推荐系统中计算效率与建模能力的矛盾，在离线指标和线上业务指标上均显著优于现有方法，并已成功大规模落地，具有极高的工业实用价值。

Abstract: Recent studies in industrial recommendation systems have demonstrated that sequential recommendation models built upon self-attention can benefit from predictable scaling laws by increasing sequence length and model capacity. However, practical recommender systems impose strict latency and resource constraints, making it challenging to balance computational overhead with fine-grained feature interaction. In this paper, we propose CCFormer, an efficient Transformer backbone that unifies cross-field feature interaction and compressed long-sequence modeling for industrial recommendation. Specifically, CCFormer combines feature-field separated cross attention with long-sequence subspace token mixing to exploit long-term preference signals across heterogeneous feature domains. A hierarchical sequence compression strategy with progressively expanded receptive fields enables efficient long-sequence modeling with reduced information loss. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that CCFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Online A/B tests in a video recommendation scenario and an advertising ranking scenario at Tencent further validate its industrial practicality, yielding a 3.57% CTR gain and a 1.71% advertising revenue lift, respectively, while accelerating model training by 2.21x over the strong HSTU baseline. CCFormer has been fully deployed in Tencent's production recommendation system, serving the main traffic of both scenarios.

</details>


### [296] [TCA-SIR: Learning Target-Conditioned Abstractions for Scientific Inspiration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28498)
*Yuto Suzuki,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的科学灵感检索方法TCA-SIR，将SIR重新定义为目标条件抽象，通过学习生成针对目标问题的可迁移抽象原则，显著提升了检索性能并提供可解释的迁移理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SIR方法仅基于主题相似度排序论文，未显式建模候选灵感如何迁移至目标问题，尤其对远程灵感（价值在于可复用的问题解决原则而非主题重叠）效果有限。受人类抽象源问题可迁移方面并映射到新目标的启发，作者将SIR重构为目标条件抽象（TCA）。

Method: 提出TCA-SIR框架：学习生成目标条件抽象，利用其表示预测迁移性。检索对象为针对特定目标从候选中提取的可迁移抽象原则。在ResearchBench上评估，对比先前SIR方法和直接LLM检索。

Result: TCA-SIR在HitRate@top4%指标上比MOOSE-Chem提升超过10个百分点，优于现有SIR方法和直接LLM检索。学习到的抽象比未训练的TCA提示词更清晰地恢复目标相关机制，实现了更强的检索性能和可解释的科学灵感理由。

Conclusion: 将SIR重新定义为目标条件抽象，通过学习生成针对目标的可迁移抽象原则，能有效捕捉远程灵感的迁移价值，显著提升检索性能并提供可解释性，为AI for Science中的假设生成提供了更有效的灵感检索范式。

Abstract: Scientific hypothesis generation for AI for Science typically involves Scientific Inspiration Retrieval (SIR) followed by hypothesis composition. Existing SIR methods rank papers by topical similarity and do not explicitly represent how a candidate inspiration transfers to a target problem. This is especially limiting for remote inspirations, whose value often lies in reusable problem-solving principles rather than topical overlap. Motivated by how humans abstract transferable aspects of a source and remap them to a new target, we reformulate SIR as target-conditioned abstraction (TCA). The retrieval object is a transferable abstract principle extracted from a candidate specifically for the target. We present TCA-SIR, which learns to generate target-conditioned abstractions and uses their representations to predict transferability. On ResearchBench, TCA-SIR outperforms prior SIR methods and direct LLM retrieval, improving HitRate@top4% over MOOSE-Chem by more than 10 percentage points. Learned abstractions also recover target-relevant mechanisms more clearly than an untrained TCA prompt, yielding both stronger retrieval and an interpretable rationale for scientific inspiration.

</details>


### [297] [MagicSelector: Joint Optimization for Agent Tool Selection via Counterfactual Decomposition and Progressive Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17751)
*HONOR Agentic Search Team,Zhengzong Chen,Lei Tang,Lijun Liu,Chuandi Jiang,Fan Yang,Keyun Chu,Chu Zhao,Shihao Liu,Minghang Li,Bo Liang,Can Wen,Hailong Wu,Jingnan Ju,Mian Liu,Nengbin Zhang,Peiqiang Wang,Penghe Nie,Qinhui Gu,Sijia Lv,Siqi Chen,Wei Zhang,Yang Xu,Yuhao Qian,Yuxiang Zhang,Zeng Cheng,Zhen Wang,Zuan Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MagicSelector是一个用于智能体工具检索的联合优化框架，融合反事实任务分解、渐进式重排序和动态Top-K策略，有效解决模糊指令转原子子任务、高精度检索及OOD场景下的噪声干扰问题，在MTDTool基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对智能体工具检索中用户指令模糊、原子子任务分解困难、高相似工具难以精准区分、长尾噪声干扰及OOD泛化能力不足等核心挑战，提出统一框架从任务分解、重排序、候选截断三个维度联合优化。

Method: 包含三大核心模块：(1)偏好引导的反事实任务分解机制，利用反事实奖励量化分解对检索排序的边际因果增益，施加细粒度结构监督保证逻辑连贯；(2)基于自蒸馏硬负例挖掘的渐进式工具重排序，联合优化点式与列式相关性，增强高相似工具间的细粒度判别；(3)双语义边界感知动态Top-K策略，自适应监测重排序分数断崖与工具间语义漂移，动态截断候选列表以最大化相关工具召回并过滤长尾噪声。

Result: 在首个面向移动多轮交互且含过程级标注的MTDTool基准上，MagicSelector在工具检索准确率、OOD泛化能力及整体token效率三个维度均显著优于最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: MagicSelector通过反事实分解、渐进重排序与动态Top-K的联合优化，有效缓解了工具检索中的语义模糊、噪声干扰与泛化瓶颈，为智能体工具调用提供了更鲁棒高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MagicSelector, a joint optimization framework integrating Counterfactual task decomposition, Progressive reranking, and Dynamic Top-K, designed to address the fundamental challenges of tool retrieval in agents. MagicSelector is a specialized framework capable of translating ambiguous user instructions into executable atomic subtasks and guiding high-precision tool retrieval, effectively mitigating redundant noise and severe context distraction in out-of-domain (OOD) scenarios. We empower MagicSelector with these capabilities through three key contributions: (1) a preference-guided counterfactual task decomposition mechanism that utilizes a counterfactual reward to quantify the marginal causal gain of decomposition on retrieval ranking, effectively imposing fine-grained structural supervision on logical coherence; (2) a progressive tool reranking method driven by self-distillation hard negative mining, which optimizes both point-wise and list-wise relevance to enhance fine-grained discrimination among highly similar tools; and (3) a dual semantic boundary-aware dynamic Top-K strategy that adaptively monitors reranking score cliffs and inter-tool semantic shifts to dynamically truncate the candidate list, maximizing relevant tool recall while filtering long-tail noise. Evaluated on MTDTool, the first task decomposition benchmark we constructed tailored for mobile multi-turn interactions with process-level annotations, MagicSelector yields promising performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MagicSelector significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of tool retrieval accuracy, OOD generalization capability, and overall token efficiency, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [298] [Measuring Alignment With Reader Highlights Net of Position and Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27739)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于自然社交高亮的上下文压缩评估方法，通过匹配相同相对深度和长度排名的句子并校准合成零假设，消除了位置和长度偏差。在120篇网页文档上，语言模型重要性排序显著优于随机基线，表现接近人类读者，且经典启发式方法也有一定效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩评估依赖下游任务准确率，存在循环评判问题。自然社交高亮提供非循环参考，但直接使用群众标记比例受位置偏差（前置）和长度偏差（长句更易被标记）混淆。需要去除这些混淆因素，建立公平评估基准。

Method: 将每个被标记句子与同文档中相同相对深度和文内长度排名的未标记句子匹配；构建仅基于位置和长度的合成零假设校准估计器；使用精确随机化检验（无聚类假设）评估显著性；在120篇网页文档（每篇≥12独立读者）上实验，跨供应商复现。

Result: LM重要性排序保留38.4%群众标记句子 vs 19.9%匹配邻居，富集度+0.196 [+0.148, +0.239]，p=0.0005。朴素截断仅+0.003。单人类读者达+0.182，与GPT-5.4无显著差异（+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]），低于Claude Opus 5。Luhn 1958启发式达+0.088。词频统计部分可恢复读者选择，词汇中心性仅额外解释0.010。先前工作中的一个声明在此语料上不复现。

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架有效消除了位置和长度混淆，证明LM重要性排序能显著捕捉人类阅读关注点，表现接近人类水平。经典启发式方法仍有预测力，说明读者选择部分可通过简单词频恢复，而非仅靠词汇中心性。该方法为上下文压缩评估提供了更严谨的非循环基准。

Abstract: Context compression discards most of a document before a language model reads it, and is normally evaluated by downstream task accuracy - which makes another model the judge of what mattered. Naturalistic social highlighting offers a non-circular reference: many people independently marking passages on the same page. But the obvious metric, the fraction of crowd-marked sentences a compressor keeps, is confounded twice: crowd marks are front-loaded and crowd-marked sentences are longer, so any method favouring early or long sentences scores well regardless of readers. We remove both by matching each marked sentence against unmarked sentences of the same document at equal relative depth and equal within-document length rank, and we calibrate every estimator on synthetic nulls built from position and length alone - a step that matters, since depth-only stratification returns a false positive on 20-36% of nulls containing no effect. On 120 web documents (at least 12 independent readers each), a language-model importance ranking keeps 38.4% of crowd-marked sentences against 19.9% of their matched neighbours: an enrichment of +0.196 [+0.148, +0.239], at p = 0.0005 under an exact randomization test that assumes nothing about clustering, and replicated cross-vendor. Naive truncation, whose keep rule is position, correctly falls to +0.003. To give the number a scale: scored identically, on the same budget, against a crowd label recomputed to exclude them, a single human reader reaches +0.182 - indistinguishable from GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) and below Claude Opus 5. Classical methods are not null - Luhn's 1958 heuristic reaches +0.088 - so reader selection is partly recoverable by counting words; conditioning additionally on lexical centrality removes only 0.010, so the agreement is not centrality. We also report that a claim in our own prior work does not reproduce on this corpus.

</details>


### [299] [OneShot: Index-in-Ranking with Neural Scoring for Large-Scale Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27475)
*Ziwei Li,Shuyao Li,Xufeng Cai,Xue Zou,Yiming Ma,Huiting Lu,Wujie Yan,Zhichen Zhao,Yang Lu,Zhe Wang,Rui Luo,Zhengyu Su,Dan Zhang,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneShot 是一个端到端的模型内索引学习框架，将索引学习与排序目标原生对齐，解决了检索系统中排序目标与索引结构错位的长期难题，在 Instagram 短视频推荐系统中部署，显著提升召回率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统的检索阶段需在海量候选中快速筛选，但传统方法中排序目标（优化预测与用户行为对齐）与索引效率目标（优化结构分组以实现快速搜索）存在根本错位，导致交互建模扩展受限。

Method: 提出 OneShot 全新检索框架，采用端到端的模型内索引学习，将索引学习与排序目标原生对齐，以此为结构基础，利用神经网络评分突破点积瓶颈，扩展交互建模表达能力。

Result: 在 Instagram 短视频推荐系统全面部署，用户日活、互动和时长显著提升；在运行排序量级下召回率提升 20%，同等召回水平下效率提升 10 倍。

Conclusion: OneShot 成功解决了检索系统中排序目标与索引结构错位的核心矛盾，通过端到端联合学习实现了高表达力的神经网络评分检索，并在工业级大规模系统中验证了显著的业务收益和效率提升。

Abstract: In modern recommendation systems, retrieval serves as a primary stage responsible for filtering billions of candidate items down to thousands prior to refined ranking. To make this massive search effective and efficient, the system relies on ranking accuracy and indexing efficiency. However, these two objectives are traditionally misaligned: while the former optimizes for the alignment between ranking predictions and user behavior, the latter optimizes for a structural grouping of item representations which enables fast search among billions of candidates. Thus, despite extensive efforts to scale up interaction modeling for retrieval, they remain fundamentally limited by the structural misalignment between the ranking objectives and the proximity-learned index. In this work, we address this long-standing dichotomy by proposing a new holistic retrieval framework, OneShot. It is an end-to-end, in-model index learning framework that natively aligns index learning with ranking objectives. Using this joint learning as a structural foundation, OneShot pushes the boundaries of retrieval expressiveness by scaling interaction modeling with neural scoring beyond the persistent dot-product bottleneck. OneShot is fully deployed in Instagram's industrial short-video recommendation system, driving significant wins in user daily sessions, engagement, and time-spent. Additionally, OneShot achieves a $20\%$ recall gain at the operational ranking volume and a 10x efficiency improvement at an equivalent recall level.

</details>


### [300] [Hierarchical Reranking for Scalable Financial RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27523)
*Joohyun Lee,Sungwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Hierarchical Reranker RAG框架，通过预检索优化、分层重排序架构和长上下文管理三大创新，解决大规模金融文档中混合文本-表格结构的检索难题，在FinQA、FinanceBench、ConvFinQA基准上达到NDCG@20 0.7918，并在ACM-ICAIF'24 FinanceRAG挑战赛获第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统难以处理金融领域的混合文本-表格结构及大规模文档（如10-K申报、表格披露、宏观经济报告），导致检索性能和生成可靠性不足，无法满足专家级金融推理需求。

Method: Hierarchical Reranker框架包含三大创新：1) 预检索优化——通过归一化、关键词扩展、表格转换提升查询清晰度与搜索效率；2) 分层重排序架构——采用两阶段排序机制提高检索精度；3) 长上下文管理——利用自适应输入分区与融合技术在海量上下文中保持推理准确性。

Result: 在FinQA、FinanceBench、ConvFinQA多个基准测试中，NDCG@20达到0.7918，展现优越的事实一致性；在ACM-ICAIF'24 FinanceRAG挑战赛中获得第二名，验证了系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本工作为大规模金融文档提供了一个可部署、领域优化的RAG流水线，显著提升了金融推理的准确性与可扩展性，为自动化审计报告和量化投资分析奠定基础，源代码将在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Analyzing financial documents such as 10-K filings, tabular disclosures, and macroeconomic reports demands expert reasoning and extensive time. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems often struggle to process hybrid text-table structures or the massive scale of financial documents. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Reranker, a RAG framework designed to improve retrieval performance and generative reliability across large-scale financial datasets. The system integrates three key innovations: Pre-Retrieval Optimization, enhancing query clarity and search efficiency through normalization, keyword expansion, and table transformation; Hierarchical Reranker Architecture, improving retrieval precision through a two-stage ranking mechanism; and Long-Context Management, preserving reasoning accuracy through adaptive input partitioning and fusion under extensive contexts. Across multiple benchmarks, including FinQA, FinanceBench, and ConvFinQA, the proposed system achieved an NDCG@20 score of 0.7918 and demonstrated superior factual consistency. Its robustness was further validated by achieving second place in the ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge. This work presents a deployable, domain-optimized RAG pipeline that enhances both the accuracy and scalability of financial reasoning, paving the way for automated audit reporting and quantitative investment analysis. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [301] [A Structured Knowledge Infrastructure for Domain-Specific Data Asset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27748)
*Mengdi Chen,Yuanxin Huang,Yulin Jiang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种双层解决方案，解决企业数据分析代理中通用RAG检索错误资产且缺乏使用知识的问题，通过三级双用途知识库、图引导检索器和场景感知排序器，在小红书广告数据仓库实现Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%，知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据分析代理面临两大结构性失败：通用RAG检索错误资产（Hit@10仅19.1%）且无法提供使用知识防止指标误读，根因包括语义鸿沟、实体歧义、模式漂移和资产-使用鸿沟。

Method: 部署在小红书商业广告数据仓库（5300+ Hive表，14个域）的双层方案：1) 三级双用途知识库（179文档，八节标注模板）服务检索与生成，配闭环刷新流水线保持天级新鲜度；2) 图引导检索器（GGR）利用2859节点知识图谱作为候选门控并意图路由，实现71.6倍token压缩；3) 场景感知排序器（SAR）应用19类实体识别与显式场景标注，负样本知识单独贡献25个百分点Hit@10提升。

Result: 在两个100题基准上，Hit@10从19.1%升至96.6%（+77.5pp），知识覆盖率从56%升至77%，端到端延迟4.84-5.33秒。

Conclusion: 该双层架构结合知识图谱门控、意图路由、场景感知排序及负样本知识，有效解决了企业数据分析中RAG检索不准和缺乏使用知识的问题，实现了高准确率、高覆盖率和低延迟的实用化部署。

Abstract: Enterprise data analytics agents face two structural failures: generic RAG retrieves the wrong asset (Hit@10=19.1%) and delivers no usage knowledge to prevent metric misinterpretation---stemming from four root causes (C1--C4) ranging from semantic gap and entity ambiguity to schema drift and asset-usage gap. We present a two-layer solution deployed in the commercial advertising data warehouse at Xiaohongshu (5,300+ Hive tables, 14 domains). A three-tier dual-purpose knowledge base (179 documents, eight-section annotation template) serves both retrieval and generation, with a closed-loop refresh pipeline maintaining day-level freshness (one yes/no approval, 30s hot-reload). The Graph-Guided Retriever (GGR) uses a 2,859-node knowledge graph as a candidate gate with intent routing to deliver 71.6x token reduction. The Scene-Aware Ranker (SAR) applies 19-class entity recognition and explicit scenario annotations; negative knowledge alone contributes 25 percentage points of Hit@10 gain. On two 100-question benchmarks, Hit@10 rises from 19.1% to 96.6% (+77.5pp) and knowledge coverage from 56% to 77%, at 4.84--5.33s end-to-end latency.

</details>
